Per DEC-INSTRUMENT-UNIVERSE-FAST-001 the lean store persists ONLY the ~236 underlyings, never the legs — so the lookup always returned `None`, every contract was dropped as `skipped_no_metadata`, and the persisted count was 0 on every cell. **Fix:** cli.py now uses the already-tested `contracts.store_metadata_lookup(store)`, which reduces the leg to its underlying via `underlying_symbol_of` BEFORE the O(1) point lookup (the helper that exists precisely for the lean store — COMMON, no duplication, CLAUDE.md §11). **Observability (the why-was-it-hidden fix):** `universe.contracts.pulled` now logs `raw` (symbols Groww returned) + `skipped_no_metadata` + `skipped_malformed` alongside the persisted `contracts` count, so a 0 is never ambiguous again — `raw>0` ⇒ a drop, `raw=0` ⇒ an empty vendor response (DEC-RUN-LOG-CAPTURE-001 extended). Covered by the existing `store_metadata_lookup` + `ContractFetcher.fetch` tests (`test_drives_a_real_fetch`, `test_inherits_from_underlying`, `underlying_symbol_of` suite); the cli wiring is coverage-excluded glue. **Decision ID:** DEC-CONTRACTS-META-FIX-001. **Builds on:** DEC-INSTRUMENT-UNIVERSE-FAST-001 (#160) · DEC-UNIVERSE-PULL-2020-001 (#317) · DEC-RUN-LOG-CAPTURE-001.

317. **DEC-UNIVERSE-PULL-2020-001 — automated full expiry+contract pull from Jan 2020, total pull-process logging, the /coverage threading crash, and /db paging** (operator runs 2026-05-30, post-#316 merge: the structured logs from #316 worked — the operator could finally copy them — which exposed the next layer of real defects). Five fixes, all on the priority law (Correctness > Reliability > Recoverability > Observability > Performance > Scalability):

    - **(A) DEC-STORE-THREADSAFE-001 — the `/coverage` 500 crash.** `GET /api/v1/coverage` 500'd with `ValueError: not enough values to unpack (expected 26, got 1)` (`_row_to_instrument`): the dashboard shares ONE `InstrumentStore` (one DuckDB connection) across the uvicorn request threads AND the background universe-pull thread (DEC-AUTO-UNIVERSE-001 reuses the single writer). A DuckDB connection has ONE result cursor, so a request reading `instruments` mid-`refresh()` (upsert + prune) garbled the row. Fixed by a `_StoreMeta` metaclass that wraps EVERY public `InstrumentStore` method in a per-store `threading.RLock` (re-entrant; execute→fetch is now atomic), plus a `_LockedReadConn` returned by `.connection` so the whitebox `/db` reader holds the same lock across execute→fetch. Proven by `TestThreadSafety::test_concurrent_read_during_upsert_prune_never_corrupts` (1 writer churning upsert+prune across snapshots + 3 readers hammering `list_index_equity_instruments`/`has_placeholder_stock_economics`/`list_coverage`; 0 errors, lake stays clean). All 79 existing store tests still pass — the lock is transparent.

    - **(B) DEC-UNIVERSE-PULL-2020-001 — automated, ALL years from 2020.** The on-run auto-pull (cli.py) and the `/coverage` "Re-pull gaps" (app.py) both pulled `years=[current_year]` only — so only 2026 expiries appeared and contracts stayed 0. Both now pull `range(2020, now+1)`: for every underlying (every admitted index + every F&O-eligible stock), every year 2020→now, every expiry → all contracts, fully automatic on run (Groww serves F&O from 2020, CLAUDE.md §6). Historical CANDLES stay manual + per-symbol by design (operator decree) — a from/to-date candle picker is the tracked follow-up.

    - **(C) DEC-RUN-LOG-CAPTURE-001 (extended) — the WHOLE pull is now in the log file.** The pull loop logged only its final outcome, so the operator (rightly) saw no per-step trail. `_run_live_universe_ingest` now emits structured events for every step — `universe.master.refreshing/refreshed`, `universe.pull.start`, `universe.cell.start` (with `N/total` progress), `universe.contracts.pulled` (per expiry), `universe.cell.done`, `universe.cell.failed` (with the error), `universe.auth.reminted`, `universe.pull.done` — all landing in `.brutex-logs/brutex.log` (JSON) + the `/logs` viewer. Plus a stdout mirror so the Groww SDK's raw `Ready to Groww!` print (and any third-party stdout) is captured to the file too ("every nook and corner").

    - **(D) auth re-mint mid-pull.** The operator's pull failed partway with "Authentication failed. Your API token has either expired or is invalid." — a long ALL-underlyings × 2020→now pull outlives one short-lived Groww access token (resets daily 6 AM IST, CLAUDE.md §6). The pull now detects an auth error per cell, re-mints ONCE via `GrowwAuth.refresh()`, rebinds the expiry/contract fetchers, and retries the cell instead of failing every remaining one.

    - **(E) DEC-DB-PAGINATION-001 — see all rows + all 26 columns in `/db`.** The `/db` viewer only ever fetched page 1 (no `offset`); the backend already returned `total`/`offset`. Added Prev/Next paging (the grid already scrolls horizontally for the wide 26-column tables) so the operator can walk every row.

    **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** the live Groww pull (whether 2020 data actually returns, the contracts=0 question) is operator-side + network-gated — this PR makes the pull cover all years, log every step (so the operator can SEE exactly what each `get_contracts` returns), and survive token expiry; the live yes/no is then visible in the log. The full 2020→now pull is inherently long (hundreds of underlyings × 7 years × many expiries × contracts, rate-limited at the adapter) — not claimed O(1); it is the documented bulk ETL. `cli.py` + dashboard routes/templates are coverage-excluded glue; the real new logic (the store lock) is proven by a concurrency stress test + the unchanged 79-test store suite. **Decision ID:** DEC-UNIVERSE-PULL-2020-001 (umbrella) with sub-IDs DEC-STORE-THREADSAFE-001 · DEC-DB-PAGINATION-001. **Builds on:** DEC-RUN-FIXPACK-001 (#316) · DEC-AUTO-UNIVERSE-001 (#314) · DEC-COVERAGE-LEDGER-001 (#166) · CLAUDE.md §6 / §13.

316. **DEC-RUN-FIXPACK-001 — six real-run defects fixed + proven against the operator's REAL 152,444-row `instrument.csv`** (operator runs 2026-05-30: repeated `make run` still showed "0 stocks", `lot_size=1`/blank `freeze_quantity`, an empty `/logs` page, an opaque "Groww API Error 400: Bad Request", the `instruments` table growing 236 → 472 on a second-day reload, and no way to verify saved credentials inside the IDE). All six are root-caused, fixed, tested, and **verified end-to-end by loading the operator's actual uploaded CSV** (RELIANCE → lot=500/tick=0.05/freeze=15001, NIFTY → lot=65/freeze=1801, 25 indices · 211 stocks, 236 lean rows, 2.8 s parse). Priority law honoured (Correctness > Reliability > Recoverability > Observability > Performance > Scalability).

    - **(A) DEC-STALE-ECONOMICS-HEAL-001 — `lot_size`/`tick_size`/`freeze_quantity` stuck at the master placeholders.** The economics back-fill (DEC-UNDERLYING-ECONOMICS-001 / #164) was correct, but the auto-heal gate (`should_reload_instrument_master`, #315) only reloaded on empty / index-less / non-lean lakes — NOT on a lake loaded *before* the back-fill landed. So a lake with `lot_size=1` placeholders served them forever. New cheap probe `InstrumentStore.has_placeholder_stock_economics()` (indexed `EXISTS` over the latest snapshot: any NSE `EQ` with `lot_size <= 1` — every real F&O stock has lot > 1) feeds a new `stale_economics` arm of `should_reload_instrument_master`. A reload now re-enriches every stock; the probe then reads clean, so the heal fires at most once (idempotent).

    - **(B) DEC-INSTRUMENT-LEAN-SNAPSHOT-001 — `instruments` table grew 236 → 472 across days (read as a broken dedup guarantee).** Each keyless load stamps a new UTC `snapshot_date`, and `upsert_many(replace_snapshot=True)` only wipes the *same* day — so a reload on a later day ADDED a second full ~236-row copy (latest-per-symbol reads stayed correct, but the raw `/db` view doubled). New `InstrumentStore.prune_to_latest_snapshot()` (collapse to the newest `snapshot_date`) runs after every keyless refresh, keeping the lake lean + unique at ~241 rows. Idempotent + safe on empty/single-snapshot lakes.

    - **(C) DEC-RUN-LOG-CAPTURE-001 — the `/logs` page + `.brutex-logs/brutex.log` were always empty.** uvicorn's loggers ship with `propagate=False` + their own stderr handlers, so HTTP/startup logs never reached our rotating file handler; and the `cmd_dashboard` milestones were `print()`s, not structured logs. Fix: `uvicorn.run(..., log_config=None)` so uvicorn's access/error loggers PROPAGATE into our root handlers, `configure_logging(json_renderer=False)` (pretty console, JSON file), and the run-path milestones + failures now emit via `structlog` (so they land in the file + the live viewer). Validated by running a real uvicorn server: brutex events + access logs + the autopull failure all captured as JSON.

    - **(D) DEC-CREDS-NORMALISE-001 — the opaque Groww 400 + the "no expiry?" confusion.** The 400 originates in `GrowwAPI.get_access_token` (HARD-VERIFIED in the wheel: `Authorization: Bearer <api_key>` + `{"key_type":"totp","totp":<code>}` → HTTP 400 string "Groww API Error 400: …"). A pasted API key with a trailing newline → malformed Bearer header → 400; a base32 TOTP secret pasted in spaced groups (Groww shows `MPB2 AAWM …`) → undecodable by `pyotp` → wrong code → 400. New `normalise_api_key` (strip) + `normalise_totp_secret` (remove ALL whitespace + upper-case canonical base32) applied on BOTH save + resolve. Saving credentials now also `clear_cached_token()`s any access token minted from the OLD creds (Groww **resets API keys daily at 6 AM IST** — the TOTP *secret* has no expiry, but the API-key JWT/access token does, so a stale cache would keep failing). The autopull failure path now logs an ACTIONABLE hint (regenerate the TOTP Token + re-save) instead of an opaque "Bad Request".

    - **(E) DEC-CREDS-PREVIEW-001 — "is it loading what I pasted, or empty?" + "I can't see credentials inside IntelliJ".** The credentials file lives at `~/.brutex/credentials.json` (outside the repo, by security design — never committed), so it is invisible in the project tree. New `credentials_preview()` returns a MASKED, non-secret `head…tail` + length of each stored field; the `/api/v1/credentials/status` endpoint + Credentials page now show it (e.g. `key eyJraW…WXYZ (912 chars) · totp MP…A2 (32 chars)`) so the operator can VERIFY the paste landed without the UI ever echoing a usable secret.

    - **(F) DEC-RUN-FIXPACK-001 — "0 stocks" startup tally.** `cmd_dashboard` summarised via `universe_summary(load_snapshot(...))` with NO `fno_eligible` set; on a lean lake (no legs) every equity fell through unclassified → "25 indices · 0 stocks" (the DATA was fine — `/universe` already showed 211). Now uses the lean-correct `universe_summary(list_index_equity_instruments(), fno_eligible=fno_eligible_underlyings())` at both the count-message and auto-pull-gate call sites — the same path the keyless loader already used.

    **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** the 10 s "Load instruments" wait is the **22 MB CSV network download**, not the parse (measured 2.8 s for 152k rows) — it is an O(n) one-per-day ETL, not an O(1) op, and is not claimed to be. The live Groww auth (the 400's ultimate yes/no) is operator-side: if it still 400s after a freshly-generated TOTP Token is re-saved, Groww is rejecting the key itself. `cli.py` + the dashboard template/routes stay coverage-excluded glue; the real logic (`has_placeholder_stock_economics`, `prune_to_latest_snapshot`, `should_reload_instrument_master` stale-arm, `normalise_*`, `credentials_preview`, `clear_cached_token`) is 100% line+branch tested (full unit+integration suite green: 2482 passed / 18 skipped; ruff clean). **Decision ID:** DEC-RUN-FIXPACK-001 (umbrella) with sub-IDs DEC-STALE-ECONOMICS-HEAL-001 · DEC-INSTRUMENT-LEAN-SNAPSHOT-001 · DEC-RUN-LOG-CAPTURE-001 · DEC-CREDS-NORMALISE-001 · DEC-CREDS-PREVIEW-001. **Builds on:** DEC-UNDERLYING-ECONOMICS-001 (#164) · DEC-INSTRUMENT-UNIVERSE-FAST-001 (#160) · DEC-INSTRUMENT-BULK-UPSERT-001 (#315) · DEC-COVERAGE-LEDGER-001 (#166) · DEC-CREDENTIALS-STORE-001 (#312) · CLAUDE.md §6 (Groww facts) · §13 (Merge-Readiness Charter).

315. **DEC-INSTRUMENT-BULK-UPSERT-001 — ultra-fast instrument-master load + auto-heal on stale lake** (operator finding 2026-05-29: `make run` still showed "0 INDEX · 211 stocks" AFTER the #313 index-parse fix merged, and loading/clicking "Load instruments" was painfully slow — "why so much struggle, our aim is O(1) ultra-fast"). **Two root causes, both fixed:** **(A) Stale lake** — the #187/#313 on-run auto-load only fired when the lake was *wholly empty* (`summary["total"] == 0`); an operator whose lake was populated by a PRE-#313 build (which quarantined all 30 index rows) had 211 stocks + 0 indices, so the guard said "present" and never re-loaded → the operator saw stale, index-less data and (wrongly) read it as the fix failing. Fix: a new pure gate `brutex/dashboard/auto_pull.py::should_reload_instrument_master(index_count, stock_count)` reloads when the lake is empty OR **missing indices** (`index_count == 0`) — the exact fingerprint of a pre-fix lake; reload is idempotent (the upsert dedups on `vendor_symbol`, DEC-INDEX-PARSE-FIX-001 / #313). 100% line+branch, all 4 states tested. **(B) Slow load (the O(1) claim audited honestly per Golden Rule #10)** — `upsert_many` did one `DELETE` + one `INSERT` **per row** (~2·N round-trips; ~10+ min for the ~150k-row master). Per-key dedup IS O(1) (the `(vendor_symbol, snapshot_date)` PK), but the BULK load was O(N) with a ruinous constant — the prior "O(1) load" framing was over-claimed and is corrected here. **Measured, not assumed:** DuckDB `executemany` is still a per-row loop internally (~22 s for 100k — no help); a set-based `DELETE … WHERE vendor_symbol IN (?, ?, … ×100k)` is **O(N²)** (the IN-list DELETE alone measured ~10 s). The real fix is DuckDB's vectorised columnar path: build the incoming rows as ONE registered Arrow table and drive both the replace (`EXISTS` semi-join, not an IN-list) and the insert (`INSERT … SELECT`) off it — **measured ~1.9 s fresh for 100k rows (~54k rows/s, ~300× the per-row loop)**, dedup + idempotent `rows_replaced` + single transaction all preserved (27 integration store tests pass unchanged; `tests/bench/test_instrument_bulk_upsert.py` pins a 30 s ceiling). **Architecture note (operator's Rust concern):** this slowness was a Python *glue* bug (a bad SQL shape), NOT a Python-vs-Rust or DuckDB limit — the same DuckDB does 257k rows/s on a clean bulk insert. The brute-force hot path (billions of combos) already runs on the Rust `fno_math` (PyO3) kernel + LMDB + Bloom with the O(1) NFR CI gate green (CLAUDE.md §4 — Python orchestration, Rust hot kernels); it never touches `upsert_many`, so this fix does not bear on the engine's latency budget. **Decision ID:** DEC-INSTRUMENT-BULK-UPSERT-001. **Builds on:** DEC-INDEX-PARSE-FIX-001 (#313, the parse fix this makes visible + fast) · DEC-INSTRUMENT-LOAD-001 (#311, the keyless loader) · DEC-AUTO-UNIVERSE-001 (#314, the sibling auto-pull in the same `cmd_dashboard` block) · CLAUDE.md §4 (Python/Rust split) · §5 O(1) discipline.

314. **DEC-AUTO-UNIVERSE-001 — on-run auto-pull of expiries + contracts, credentials-gated and non-blocking** (operator goal 2026-05-29: per CLAUDE.md §11 AUTOMATED, when the app starts and Groww credentials are present, the full universe of expiries + contracts should pull automatically with zero clicks — the keyless instrument master already auto-loads on run per DEC-INDEX-PARSE-FIX-001 / #313, but expiries and contracts need Groww auth (CLAUDE.md §6 — `get_expiries` / `get_contracts` go through the authenticated SDK), so they could not ride the keyless path). **What landed:** `cmd_dashboard` (cli.py, coverage-excluded) gained a SECOND best-effort block AFTER the #187 keyless instrument auto-load: when `credentials_present()` (CLAUDE.md §6 — env vars OR the 0600 saved file via DEC-CREDENTIALS-STORE-001 / #312) AND the instrument master is non-empty, it launches `_run_live_universe_ingest` on a **background daemon thread** so a long live pull (≈250 underlyings × N years — CLAUDE.md §3 DEC-P0-063) never blocks the dashboard from serving, and a network/rate-limit failure (Groww Data APIs 5 r/s + 100k/day, CLAUDE.md §6) is caught so it never crashes the server. **Candles stay a manual pull** (no scope expansion — CLAUDE.md §3). The pure GO/NO-GO decision is extracted to `brutex/dashboard/auto_pull.py::should_auto_pull_universe(credentials_present, instrument_total)` (100% line+branch, all 4 combinations tested) so the gate logic is real tested code while only the live-SDK/thread glue stays `# pragma: no cover`. **Concurrency decision (verified, not assumed):** the dashboard holds an open read-write `InstrumentStore` (DuckDB single file). A throwaway in-process probe showed `InstrumentStore.open` (which does `duckdb.connect(str(path))`, default read-write) DOES allow a second handle to the same file in-process without locking — but two independent read-write handles do not coordinate write transactions cleanly, so option (b) was chosen: `_run_live_universe_ingest` gained an optional `store: Any = None` param — when injected (dashboard path) it REUSES the dashboard's single open store and does NOT close it; when `None` (the unchanged `fetch --universe` path) it opens/closes its own. One writer, no ambiguity. Backward-compatible.

313. **DEC-INDEX-PARSE-FIX-001 — index (IDX) rows now parse: the dashboard's "0 INDEX" was the parser quarantining all 30 index rows of the real Groww master** (operator finding 2026-05-29: `/universe` shows "0 INDEX" even after loading the master, while 211 stocks load fine). CONFIRMED against the operator's REAL `instrument.csv`: the master carries **30 `IDX` rows** (NIFTY, BANKNIFTY, SENSEX, INDIAVIX, FINNIFTY, BANKEX, MIDCPNIFTY/NIFTYMIDSELECT + ~23 sectoral/broad indices) and EVERY one of them failed `_row_to_instrument` on **three fields**, any one of which was fatal in the `_ROW_ERRORS` quarantine try/except: (1) `exchange_token` is the SYMBOL string for NSE indices (`"NIFTYMETAL"`, `"NIFTY"`; 24 of 30 non-numeric — BSE indices use small ints `1`/`14`) → `int(float("NIFTYMETAL"))` ValueError; (2) `lot_size` BLANK for all 30 → `int(float(""))` ValueError; (3) `tick_size` BLANK for all 30 → `normalise_tick_size("")` TickSizeFormatError. Indices are reference instruments — never directly tradable (`brutex/universe.py`) — so their economics are legitimately absent.

    **What landed:**
    - **`brutex/adapters/groww/instruments.py` `_row_to_instrument`** — index-tolerant for IDX only: new `_req_int_or_zero` helper maps a non-numeric/blank `exchange_token` → `0` (metadata, NOT the dedup key — `vendor_symbol` is primary; tolerant for ALL rows, only index rows hit the non-numeric branch in practice); `lot_size` → `0` and `tick_size` → `Decimal("0")` when blank-on-index; EQ/FUT/CE/PE stay STRICT (a blank lot/tick or bad token on a tradable row still quarantines).
    - **`brutex/models.py`** — added the `is_index` property (IDX only); `Instrument.__post_init__` lot/tick guards relaxed for indices (`< 0` rejected, `== 0` allowed) while tradable types keep `<= 0` rejection.
    - **`brutex/execution/types.py` `InstrumentMeta.__post_init__`** — the SAME guard surfaced again in the execution-layer projection (`InstrumentStore.load_snapshot` builds an `InstrumentMeta` for every instrument, indices included): relaxed to allow `lot_size == 0` for IDX only (the simulator never sizes an order against an index). This was the path actually hit by the dashboard's real-store load — the originally-broken surface.
    - **`brutex/cli.py` `cmd_dashboard`** — `brutex run` now AUTO-LOADS the keyless public instrument master on startup when the lake is empty (best-effort; a network failure never blocks serving), so `/universe` is never empty on a fresh lake with zero clicks (CLAUDE.md §11 AUTOMATED). The existing Load-instruments button still works.

    **Tests:** real-30-row regression fixture `tests/fixtures/groww_idx_rows_real.csv` (header + all 30 real IDX rows) drives `test_real_index_rows_all_parse` (asserts parsed==30, quarantined==0); `_req_int_or_zero` table test; both-branch tests proving EQ/option strictness is preserved; dashboard-loader real-style IDX row; models `is_index` True/False; `InstrumentMeta` IDX zero-lot allowed + negative-lot rejected.

    **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** ✅ REAL — 100% line+branch on `instruments.py` / `instrument_loader.py` / `execution/types.py` / `models.py`; mypy --strict + ruff + bandit clean; full `tests/unit` green (2243 passed, 1 pre-existing unrelated skip). 🟡 The auto-load + live HTTP fetch path is `# pragma: no cover` (network-blocked CI; exercised on the operator's Mac). 📋 INDIA VIX presence in the live master remains Phase 0b HARD-GATE unverified (CLAUDE.md §6); the fixture is the operator's pasted real export, not a live probe.

    **Decision ID:** DEC-INDEX-PARSE-FIX-001. **Builds on:** DEC-INSTRUMENT-LOAD-001 (entry #311, keyless load) · DEC-VENDOR-PIVOT-001 (Groww facts) · CLAUDE.md §6 (index symbol formats).

312. **DEC-CREDENTIALS-STORE-001 — secure local credentials store: operator sets the Groww API key + TOTP secret ONCE; they auto-load every session** (operator goal 2026-05-29: stop `export BRUTEX_GROWW_*` per shell — per CLAUDE.md §11 AUTOMATED a one-time set must persist with zero clicks per day). A MECHANISM-only change: a hardened, secrets-never-logged file store plus the three set/load surfaces (`brutex auth set`, the dashboard Credentials page, and the existing `BRUTEX_GROWW_*` env vars), wired so the live auth + the dashboard's dry-run gate both honour a saved file.

    **What landed:**
    - **New module `brutex/config/credentials.py`** — `GrowwCredentials` (frozen slots dataclass), `CredentialsError`, `credentials_path` (`BRUTEX_CREDENTIALS_FILE` override → `~/.brutex/credentials.json` default), `save_groww_credentials` (atomic 0600 write, 0700 parent), `load_groww_credentials` (missing → `None`; insecure-perms / corrupt / wrong-shape / blank → `CredentialsError`), `resolve_groww_credentials` (PER-FIELD env-first, file-fallback), `credentials_present` (boolean; insecure-file → `False`, never raises), `clear_groww_credentials`. stdlib-only. **100% line + branch** (`coverage report` → `106 stmts / 0 miss / 34 branch / 0 BrPart / 100.00%`), zero `# pragma: no cover`. mypy --strict clean, ruff/bandit/interrogate clean.
    - **Wired `brutex/adapters/groww/auth.py::GrowwAuth.from_env`** (`# pragma: no cover` live wiring) to source `api_key` / `totp_secret` from `resolve_groww_credentials()` instead of raw `os.environ[...]` — env still wins per field, file fills the rest. `BRUTEX_GROWW_TOKEN_PATH` handling unchanged.
    - **Wired `brutex/dashboard/pull_controller.py::groww_creds_present`** to delegate to `credentials_present(env)` so a saved FILE (not only env vars) flips the dashboard pull route off dry-run. Signature unchanged; existing creds-present tests made HERMETIC (each `False` case points `BRUTEX_CREDENTIALS_FILE` at a nonexistent path so a developer's real `~/.brutex/credentials.json` can't contaminate CI).
    - **CLI `brutex auth set|status|clear`** (coverage-excluded glue) — `set` prompts via `getpass.getpass()` (NO echo, NEVER argv flags → no shell-history / `ps` leak), saves, and prints only `credentials saved to <path> (mode 0600)`; `status` prints `set`/`not set` + path; `clear` removes the file. Mirrors the `audit` subparser-with-subcommands pattern.
    - **Dashboard routes** (coverage-excluded) — `POST /api/v1/credentials` (Pydantic `CredentialsIn` BODY model, never Query; `CredentialsError` → HTTP 400 with NO value in the message; body NOT logged; returns `{"present": true}`), `GET /api/v1/credentials/status` (`{"present": bool}` only), `GET /credentials` (new `templates/credentials.html`, v28-hybrid dark page with two `type=password` inputs, an `esc()`-escaped status/message line, and the "stored locally / never committed / rotate" note). A "Credentials" nav link added to `universe.html`.
    - **`.gitignore`** safety net — `credentials.json`, `*.credentials.json`, `.brutex/` (the real default lives OUTSIDE the repo at `~/.brutex`, but the repo is guarded too).

    **Security invariants (the only thing this module is for):** (1) no secret VALUE is ever logged / printed / echoed — error messages name WHICH field, never its value; (2) file written mode 0600, parent 0700, ATOMICALLY (temp file in the same dir → chmod 0600 → `os.replace`, no world-readable window, temp cleaned on failure); (3) on READ a file with any group/world bits (`mode & 0o077`) is REFUSED → `chmod 600` (mirrors `auth._INSECURE_MODE_BITS`); (4) secrets NEVER enter `DashboardState` / `~/.brutex/runtime.json` — status surfaces return booleans only; (5) credentials accepted in request BODY only, never query params.

    **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** the real deliverable is the tested mechanism module + its three wirings. The CLI handlers, the FastAPI routes, the HTML template, and `from_env` are coverage-excluded glue (interactive / live-network / browser), consistent with the existing `# pragma: no cover` discipline for those surfaces. No claim is made that this audits the secret at rest beyond filesystem 0600 perms; full-disk encryption / OS keychain integration is out of scope (a possible future decision).

    **Decision ID:** DEC-CREDENTIALS-STORE-001. **Builds on:** DEC-VENDOR-PIVOT-001 (CLAUDE.md §6 TOTP-ACCEPTED auth) · DEC-INSTRUMENT-LOAD-001 (#311).

311. **DEC-INSTRUMENT-LOAD-001 — keyless "Load instruments" capability: populate the `/universe` list from the public instrument master with NO Groww keys** (operator finding 2026-05-29: `/universe` shows "0 INDEX / 0 STOCKS / no matches" because nobody has loaded the instrument master, and the operator should be able to populate the index/stock LIST without keys — the master is a PUBLIC CSV; only expiries/contracts/candles need auth). Adds a thin, fully-testable seam plus a button + a CLI flag so the list can be filled keylessly.

    **What landed:**
    - **New module `brutex/dashboard/instrument_loader.py`** — `load_instrument_master(store, *, http_get=None, run_id_factory=None) -> dict`. Constructs an `InstrumentMasterFetcher` (real keyless public-CSV path via `InstrumentMasterFetcher.from_env(store)` when `http_get` is `None` — `# pragma: no cover`, live network; injected fake `http_get` for offline tests), calls `.refresh()`, reads back `store.load_snapshot(in_scope_only=False)`, and returns `{"parsed","filtered","quarantined","index_count","stock_count","total"}` by REUSING `universe_query.universe_summary` (COMMON — no re-derivation of the index/stock split). **NO `GrowwAuth` / creds referenced anywhere on this path.** 100% line+branch on the non-pragma path (`coverage report` shows `13 stmts / 0 miss / 0 branch / 100.00%`).
    - **Route (thin, coverage-excluded `app.py`)** — `POST /api/v1/universe/load-instruments` via `_require_store()` → `load_instrument_master(store)` → returns the dict. Maps `InstrumentFormatError` (CSV-format / parse failure) / `OSError` (network) → **HTTP 502** with a short message so the UI can show "couldn't reach Groww asset host". A non-UTF-8 / truncated body is a parse failure, so `from_env`'s SSRF-guarded fetch re-raises `UnicodeDecodeError` (a `ValueError`, NOT an `OSError`) as `InstrumentFormatError` at the decode boundary — closing the one path the keyless-load hostile review flagged as an uncaught 500. A store-integrity `CatalogError` (degenerate already-broken store) is out of scope of the "network/parse → 502" contract. Verified with a FastAPI `TestClient`: empty store + network-blocked CI → 502 (not 500); no store → 503.
    - **`/universe` UI (`templates/universe.html`)** — a "⟳ Load instruments" button in BOTH the pullbar AND the empty banner. On click → POST the endpoint, inline "loading…" state, then on success re-runs `loadSummary()` + `loadInstruments()` so the ~200 instruments appear; on error shows the `esc()`-escaped message. Matches the v28-hybrid palette.
    - **CLI (`cli.py`, coverage-excluded)** — `brutex fetch --instruments` runs ONLY the keyless master load (no auth), prints the index/stock counts, and exits. Existing `--universe` (expiries/contracts WITH keys) is unchanged.
    - **Tests `tests/unit/test_dashboard_instrument_loader.py`** — inject a fake `http_get` (CSV with 1 NSE IDX index + 1 F&O-eligible stock + its CE derivative + 1 MCX out-of-scope row) against a REAL temp `InstrumentStore`; assert parsed=3 / filtered=1 / index_count=1 / stock_count=1; malformed-header CSV raises `InstrumentFormatError`; empty CSV → all-zero counts; default `run_id_factory` path covered. ruff + ruff format + mypy --strict + pyright all GREEN; interrogate 100%.

    **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** the instrument master is **keyless/public** (`growwapi-assets.groww.in/instruments/instrument.csv`, CLAUDE.md §6) — populating the LIST needs no Groww keys. Expiries / contracts / candles **still need keys** (separate `fetch --universe` / pull paths, unchanged). The live HTTP fetch (`InstrumentMasterFetcher.from_env`) is `# pragma: no cover` (network-blocked in CI; reachable on the operator's Mac) — so the keyless populate is **proven offline with a fake `http_get`** but the real fill needs the operator's online Mac. The `app.py` route + the template are coverage-excluded (same rationale as every FastAPI route + HTML asset in this repo). **Decision ID:** DEC-INSTRUMENT-LOAD-001. **Builds on:** DEC-UNIVERSE-DASHBOARD-001 (#306, the `/universe` page + `universe_summary` classifier + v28-hybrid palette + `esc()` this reuses) · DEC-FETCH-UNIVERSE-001 (#299, the `--universe` ingest this sits beside) · the public instrument-master CSV (CLAUDE.md §6).

310. **DEC-DASHBOARD-AUTOOPEN-001 — `make run` auto-opens `/universe` + an empty-state banner explains why it's empty** (operator frustration 2026-05-29: ran `make run` three times, kept seeing "0 INDEX / 0 STOCKS", thought it was broken; "universe webpage didn't open automatically … why even logs directory and its architecture is not visible"). Two UX fixes — the app now explains itself instead of looking broken: **(A)** `cli.cmd_run` opens the browser to **`/universe`** (the operator's working surface) instead of `/`. **(B)** `templates/universe.html` shows a prominent empty-state banner when `index_count + stock_count == 0`: it states the dashboard works but the lake is empty (expected, not a bug), the exact unblock steps (allow Groww hosts + set `BRUTEX_GROWW_API_KEY`/`BRUTEX_GROWW_TOTP_SECRET`, then `brutex fetch --universe` or click→Pull), the on-disk paths (lake `~/.brutex/lake` · DB `instruments.duckdb` · logs `~/.brutex/logs/brutex.log`), and quick links to `/` · `/logs` · `/db`. **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** this is pure UX — it does NOT make data appear; the universe stays empty until the operator unblocks Groww + ingests (the one remaining wall, on the operator side per DEC-FETCH-UNIVERSE-001 §0). `cli.py` + the template are coverage-excluded glue (no new logic module). **Decision ID:** DEC-DASHBOARD-AUTOOPEN-001. **Builds on:** DEC-UNIVERSE-DASHBOARD-001 (#306) · DEC-UNIVERSE-STORE-FIX-001 (#307) · DEC-ONECLICK-RUN-001.

309. **DEC-DB-VIEWER-001 — read-only DuckDB browser page (`/db`) + `/api/v1/db/tables` + `/api/v1/db/table/{name}` endpoints** (operator need 2026-05-29, pressed hard: "where is the DB, how can I see it, db design — every nook and corner"; today seeing the instrument-master store requires the `duckdb` CLI). Turns the 5-table DuckDB instrument store into a browsable dashboard page so the operator can inspect tables + row counts + sample rows in the browser.

    **Three-piece build:**
    - `brutex/dashboard/db_view.py` (NEW, all real logic — 100% line+branch coverage) — `KNOWN_TABLES` allowlist of the 5 real store tables (`instruments`, `instrument_runs`, `expiries`, `contracts`, `schema_meta`); `table_summary(conn)` runs one `SELECT COUNT(*)` per known table (tolerates a missing/foreign table → count 0); `sample_rows(conn, table, *, limit)` validates `table in KNOWN_TABLES` (else `ValueError`), clamps `limit` to `[1, 500]`, and issues a read-only `SELECT * FROM <validated-table> LIMIT ?` with the limit parameter-bound + every cell coerced JSON-safe (`None`→null, primitives pass through, bytes decoded, dates/Decimals str()'d); `clamp_limit(n)`.
    - `brutex/dashboard/app.py` (thin routes, coverage-excluded) — `GET /api/v1/db/tables` → `table_summary`; `GET /api/v1/db/table/{name}?limit=N` → `sample_rows` with its `ValueError` mapped to HTTP 400; `GET /db` → new template. All go through the existing `_require_store()` → `InstrumentStore.connection`.
    - `brutex/dashboard/templates/db.html` (NEW, coverage-excluded) — v28-hybrid dark page; left pane lists the 5 tables with count-up row-count badges; clicking a table fetches + renders its columns + sample rows in a monospace scrollable grid with a 10/50/200 rows selector; an empty table shows a friendly "empty — pull data to populate" note. Every column name + cell is escaped via an `esc()` helper before innerHTML (XSS — values originate from the instrument-master CSV; same lesson as `logs.html` / `universe.html`).

    **SQL-injection guard:** DuckDB cannot parameter-bind an *identifier*, so the table name is interpolated — but ONLY after exact-membership check against the `KNOWN_TABLES` allowlist; a payload like `"instruments; DROP TABLE contracts"` raises `ValueError` before any SQL is built (regression-tested: the `contracts` table survives the rejected payload). The row `limit` is parameter-bound AND clamped to `[1, 500]` so a hostile `?limit=999999999` can never trigger an unbounded read.

    **Tests:** `tests/unit/test_dashboard_db_view.py` (20 tests, REAL temp `InstrumentStore.open(tmp_path/'instruments.duckdb')`, no FastAPI dep) — empty DB → all data tables 0 + samples empty; after `upsert_many` → counts reflect + columns/rows returned with JSON-safe cells + NULL→null; unknown table → ValueError; injection payload → ValueError (not executed, contracts intact); limit clamp 0/-1/huge → [1,500]; `clamp_limit` parametrized; missing-table tolerance; `_json_safe` primitive/bytes/Decimal/date coercion. `coverage run --branch` → **100.00%** line+branch. End-to-end route wiring smoke-checked via FastAPI `TestClient` (tables/table/400/`/db`-HTML).

    **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):**
    - ✅ REAL: read-only browse of the 5 DuckDB tables works end-to-end today (verified via TestClient); table allowlist + limit clamp enforced + tested; `db_view.py` is 100% line+branch, mypy --strict + pyright clean, ruff + interrogate green.
    - 🟡 PARTIAL: `app.py` routes + `db.html` template + the live-tail JS are coverage-EXCLUDED (FastAPI-dependent / browser-only), mirroring DEC-LOGS-VIEWER-001 + DEC-UNIVERSE-DASHBOARD-001. All real logic is in the coverage-tested `db_view.py`.
    - 📋 ROADMAP: the store is **empty until data is ingested** — the lake fills only after a real Groww `fetch --universe` run, which is still network-blocked + uncredentialed (DEC-FETCH-UNIVERSE-001 §0 / DEC-UNIVERSE-STORE-FIX-001 #307). Until then `/db` correctly shows the 5 tables with the data tables at 0 rows + the "pull data to populate" note.

    **Decision ID:** DEC-DB-VIEWER-001. **Builds on:** DEC-UNIVERSE-DASHBOARD-001 (#306, the dashboard page/route/template pattern + v28-hybrid palette + `esc()` XSS helper this reuses) · DEC-LOGS-VIEWER-001 (#308, the thin-route + coverage-excluded-template + poll pattern this mirrors) · the `InstrumentStore` schema (`brutex/data/instrument_store.py`, the 5 tables + `connection` property this browses) · DEC-P0-017 (#39, the read-only `InstrumentStore.connection` accessor).

308. **DEC-LOGS-VIEWER-001 — rotating log-file sink + live `/logs` viewer page + `/api/v1/logs` tail endpoint** (operator need 2026-05-29: "where can I see the logs — there's no proper mechanism"; today logs go only to the terminal). Adds (A) an OPT-IN rotating file sink to `brutex/observability/logging.py` so structlog ALSO writes structured JSON lines to a log file, and (B) a dashboard log viewer so the operator can watch logs live in the browser.

    **What landed:**
    - `brutex/observability/logging.py` — extended `configure_logging(...)` with `log_file` / `enable_file` / `max_bytes` / `backup_count` params + a new `resolve_log_file(...)` path resolver and `_build_file_handler(...)` factory. Path resolution order: explicit arg → `$BRUTEX_LOG_FILE` → `$BRUTEX_LOG_DIR/brutex.log` → `~/.brutex/logs/brutex.log`. Uses stdlib `logging.handlers.RotatingFileHandler` (default 10 MiB × 5 backups). The file sink ALWAYS emits JSON lines (machine-parseable for the viewer + audit tooling) even when the console is in `ConsoleRenderer` mode. When the file sink is enabled, structlog is routed THROUGH stdlib logging (`LoggerFactory` + `wrap_for_formatter`) so BOTH the console AND the file receive every BruteX event — the prior `PrintLoggerFactory` path bypassed stdlib handlers and would have leaked only third-party logs to the file. The console-only path is preserved verbatim (backward-compatible). Handler swap is guarded (tagged handler + close-on-replace) so repeated calls never stack handlers or leak file descriptors. **100% line + branch** (logging.py 85 stmts / 14 branches; 6 new test classes).
    - `brutex/observability/log_tail.py` (NEW, COMMON single source) — pure `tail_lines(path, *, max_lines)` that seeks backwards from EOF in 64 KiB blocks (never slurps a multi-GB file), tolerates non-UTF-8 (`errors="replace"`), returns `[]` for a missing / non-regular / empty file, and clamps `max_lines` to `[1, 2000]` via `clamp_max_lines`. `tail_records(...)` parses JSON-log lines into dicts with a raw-line fallback (`{"event": line}`) for non-JSON / non-object lines. **100% line + branch** (54 stmts / 16 branches; 20 tests).
    - `brutex/dashboard/app.py` — thin routes: `GET /api/v1/logs?lines=N` → `JSONResponse({"lines": [...], "path": str})` over `tail_records` + `resolve_log_file`; `GET /logs` (HTMLResponse) serving the new template via a cached `_load_logs_template()` (same pattern as `_load_universe_template`).
    - `brutex/dashboard/templates/logs.html` (NEW) — v28-hybrid dark page reusing the universe palette/fonts/animations. Polls `/api/v1/logs` every 2s, colour-codes level (info/debug=blue, warning=yellow, error/critical=red), auto-scrolls to newest, has a lines selector + pause + auto-scroll toggle. Every interpolated log string is escaped via the same `esc()` helper as the universe page before innerHTML (XSS).
    - `brutex/cli.py` — `cmd_dashboard` now calls `configure_logging(json_renderer=True, enable_file=True)` so a `make run` session writes the file, and prints the resolved log path + `/logs` URL.

    **File-sink env vars:** `BRUTEX_LOG_FILE` (full path override) · `BRUTEX_LOG_DIR` (directory; file is `brutex.log` inside it) · default `~/.brutex/logs/brutex.log`.

    **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #1 + #10):**
    - ✅ COVERED 100% line+branch: the two NEW testable modules — `observability/log_tail.py` (tail reader, clamp, JSON parse + fallback, missing/empty/non-UTF-8/multi-block) and the new file-sink logic in `observability/logging.py` (path resolution, handler construction, idempotence guard, no-double-add, custom rotation params). mypy --strict clean; pyright 0 errors; ruff check + format clean; interrogate 100% docstrings; no `print` in library code; no bare except.
    - 👁️ COVERAGE-EXCLUDED (per `pyproject.toml [tool.coverage.run].omit`): `brutex/dashboard/app.py` (the 2 thin routes) and `brutex/cli.py` (the `cmd_dashboard` wiring) — exercised manually + by an offline FastAPI `TestClient` smoke run during this PR (verified: `/api/v1/logs` returns parsed records + path, clamp accepts a huge `lines=` and still 200s, `/logs` serves the page with `esc()` + polling), but not part of the automated coverage gate, same rationale as the existing dashboard/CLI exclusions.
    - 🟡 NOT UNIT-COVERABLE: the live-tail JS poll loop + the browser auto-scroll are HTML/JS, not Python — validated by manual render, not by a unit test.
    - LOCAL-ONLY: logs are persisted to a local rotating file only. NO remote / AWS / cloud log shipping is implemented or implied.

    **Decision ID:** DEC-LOGS-VIEWER-001. **Builds on:** DEC-UNIVERSE-DASHBOARD-001 (entry #306, the dashboard page/route/template pattern + v28-hybrid palette + `esc()` XSS helper this reuses) · `brutex/observability/logging.py` (the existing structlog config this EXTENDS, not duplicates).

307. **DEC-UNIVERSE-STORE-FIX-001 — `brutex dashboard`/`run` now opens a real InstrumentStore (fixes the `/universe` 500)** (operator bug report 2026-05-29: ran `make run` on the M4 Pro, opened `/universe`, saw "0 INDEX / 0 STOCKS / no matches"; server log showed `AttributeError: 'PosixPath' object has no attribute 'load_snapshot'` at `app.py:638` & `:653`). **Root cause:** `cli.py::cmd_dashboard` passed the bare `lake_root` **Path** into `build_app(..., instrument_store=lake_root)`, so `_require_store()` returned a `PosixPath` and every store-backed universe route (`/api/v1/universe/summary`, `/api/v1/universe/instruments`) 500'd. This was the exact route-layer gap the PR #180 hostile review flagged as untested (H1 — the FastAPI route test `importorskip`-skips without the `[ui]` extra, so CI never exercised the wiring). **Fix:** `cmd_dashboard` opens `InstrumentStore.open(lake_root / "instruments.duckdb")` and passes THAT; `InstrumentStore.open` runs `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS`, so a fresh/empty lake yields a valid empty store (universe shows 0 instruments, no crash) — and a real store also has the `.path` attr `_resolve_pull_lake_root` needs. **Regression test:** `tests/integration/test_universe_explorer_store.py` opens a real empty store, calls the exact `load_snapshot(in_scope_only=False)` op that 500'd, and asserts `universe_summary → 0/0` + an empty `list_underlyings`; a second test seeds a broad-market + a sectoral index (NIFTY + NIFTY IT) and asserts both classify as `index` (no FastAPI dependency → runs in every env, closing the H1 coverage hole). **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** this fixes the crash, but `/universe` still shows **0 instruments until data is ingested** — the lake is empty because Groww is still network-blocked + uncredentialed (DEC-FETCH-UNIVERSE-001 §0). The NSE indices / SENSEX / F&O stocks appear only after a real `fetch --universe` run, which needs the operator unblock. **Decision ID:** DEC-UNIVERSE-STORE-FIX-001. **Builds on:** DEC-UNIVERSE-DASHBOARD-001 (#306, the page this repairs) · DEC-CLONE-RUN-002 (#297, the `make run` path that surfaced it).

306. **DEC-UNIVERSE-DASHBOARD-001 — real F&O Universe Explorer page (`/universe`): two-kind browse (Index/Stock) + drill + click-to-pull spot** (operator directive: grow the approved clickable mockup `docs/dashboard/mockups/universe-explorer-v1.html` into a live feature). Turns the v28-hybrid mockup into a served dashboard page backed by real endpoints, with the locked design decision that the universe filter has **exactly two kinds — Index and Stock** (F&O-eligible single stocks only; a "stock" == an equity that underlies at least one NSE F&O contract). There is **NO non-F&O cash-equity tab** — a cash equity with no derivatives is simply not in the explorer.

    **What landed:**
    - `brutex/dashboard/universe_query.py` — pure, import-light classification + summary helpers. `classify_instrument(instrument, fno_eligible)` returns `"index"` for `IDX`, `"stock"` for an `EQ` whose `(exchange, trading_symbol)` is F&O-eligible, else `None` (derivative legs + non-F&O equities are excluded). `universe_summary(snapshot)` → `{index_count, stock_count, total}`; `list_underlyings(snapshot, kind=index|stock|None)` → sorted JSON rows. REUSES `brutex.universe.build_fno_eligible_underlyings` — the index-vs-stock rule lives in exactly one place (Golden Rule #11 COMMON), not re-derived. **100% line + branch** (15 tests).

      **Refinement B (operator, post-build):** the Index tab includes **ALL NSE indices**, not only the 3 F&O / brute-force ones. This was already true in code (`classify_instrument` returns `"index"` for ANY `IDX` instrument, and the snapshot loads `in_scope_only=False`); the refinement makes it **explicit** to prevent regression: the `universe_query` module + `classify_instrument` docstrings now state that `"index"` = any NSE index — sectoral (NIFTY IT/AUTO/PHARMA), broad-market (NIFTY 100/500), volatility (INDIA VIX) — NOT just NIFTY/BANKNIFTY/SENSEX; non-F&O indices are **spot-only** (no expiries/contracts, so their drill is simply empty). Two new unit tests lock the behaviour: a fake non-F&O sectoral index ("NIFTY IT") classifies as `"index"` and appears in `list_underlyings(kind="index")`.
    - `brutex/dashboard/pull_controller.py` — `pull_candles(symbol, *, lake_root, year, dry_run, fetcher_factory=...)` ingests ONE instrument's candle data across `ALL_GROWW_INTERVALS` for the year, REUSING `brutex.adapters.groww.ingest.run_ingest` (no copy-paste of the chunk/fetch spine). Offline/dry-run uses `SyntheticCandleFetcher` (no creds/network); the live Groww wiring is `_build_live_pull_fetcher` (`# pragma: no cover`, mirrors `cli._build_live_fetcher`). Returns per-timeframe completed/failed counts in `PullCandlesReport`. **100% line + branch** (8 tests).

      **Refinement A (operator, post-build):** the manual candle pull is **explicitly instrument-agnostic — spot + futures + options**. The function/report were renamed `pull_spot`→`pull_candles` / `PullSpotReport`→`PullCandlesReport` to reflect that the symbol may be a spot index/stock, a FUT, OR a CE/PE option contract's `groww_symbol` (the chunk/fetch spine is identical for any Groww symbol; signature + behaviour otherwise unchanged). The `POST /api/v1/universe/pull?symbol=&year=` route already accepted any symbol; only its docstrings were widened. Frontend `universe.html`: the spot button still pulls the selected instrument's spot, and clicking the **future row** or an **option-chain CE/PE cell** now triggers a pull of THAT contract's `groww_symbol` via the same endpoint (shared `runPull(symbol, label)` + the existing pull-animation panel, which labels which symbol — spot vs the contract — is being pulled). v28-hybrid palette + animations preserved.
    - `brutex/dashboard/app.py` (coverage-excluded) — four thin routes delegating to the modules above: `GET /api/v1/universe/summary`, `GET /api/v1/universe/instruments?kind=`, `POST /api/v1/universe/pull?symbol=` (forces `dry_run` when `BRUTEX_GROWW_API_KEY` / `BRUTEX_GROWW_TOTP_SECRET` are absent), and `GET /universe` serving `templates/universe.html`. The drill into expiries/contracts REUSES the existing `/api/v1/expiries` + `/api/v1/contracts` (no duplication).
    - `brutex/dashboard/templates/universe.html` — the mockup ported to real `fetch()` calls; synthetic JS data removed. Keeps the v28-hybrid palette + animation vocabulary, the two Index/Stock tabs, the expiry→contract drill, and the pull animation now driven by the real `POST /api/v1/universe/pull` response. The "PREVIEW · synthetic" badge shows only when the pull ran `dry_run`.
    - `brutex/dashboard/__init__.py` — made the `app` re-exports lazy (PEP 562 `__getattr__`) so importing the import-light `universe_query` / `pull_controller` submodules no longer eagerly pulls in `app` → orchestrator → `fastbloom_rs`; keeps per-module coverage runs decoupled. No public API change (no caller used the package-level re-export).

    **Hostile-review fixes (pre-merge, branch `claude/universe-dashboard`):**
    - **B1 (BLOCKER) — spot pull sends the exchange-prefixed Groww symbol.** The spot button previously POSTed the bare `trading_symbol` ("NIFTY"), which broke `symbol_to_exchange_segment` (it splits on the leading exchange token). `list_underlyings` now emits a `pull_symbol` key = `instrument.vendor_symbol` (the `NSE-`/`BSE-`-prefixed Groww symbol) alongside the display `symbol`; `universe.html` tracks `selPullSymbol` and the spot button POSTs `pull_symbol` (future/option cells already sent `contract_symbol` — unchanged). Locked by `test_row_shape` (now asserts `pull_symbol`) + `test_pull_symbol_is_exchange_prefixed` (asserts every `pull_symbol` starts with `NSE-`/`BSE-`).
    - **H1 (HIGH) — testable creds/dry-run safety gate.** The "force dry_run when no creds" decision moved out of coverage-excluded `app.py` into a pure `pull_controller.groww_creds_present(env=os.environ)` (checks `BRUTEX_GROWW_API_KEY` AND `BRUTEX_GROWW_TOTP_SECRET`, matching `GrowwAuth.from_env`). The route now calls `dry_run = not groww_creds_present()`. Both branches unit-tested (`TestGrowwCredsPresent`: present→True, absent→False, each-one-missing→False, blank→False, default-`os.environ`).
    - **H2 (HIGH) — innerHTML XSS escaping.** Added an `esc(s)` helper in `universe.html` (escapes `& < > " '`); every broker-sourced free-text string interpolated into `innerHTML` (instrument `kind`/`symbol`/`name`/`exchange`, future `contract_symbol`, pull `label`/error message, timeframe `interval`) now flows through `esc()`. Palette + animations untouched.
    - **L1 (LOW) — symbol-shape validation in `pull_candles`.** After the blank check, the symbol is rejected (`ValueError`) unless it starts with a known exchange prefix (`_KNOWN_EXCHANGE_PREFIXES = ("NSE-","BSE-")`) — a defence-in-depth guard against B1-style regressions. Locked by `test_unprefixed_symbol_raises` (reject "NIFTY") + `test_bse_prefixed_symbol_passes`.
    - **M1 (MEDIUM, live-only) — no silent lake-root guess.** `app._resolve_pull_lake_root(store, *, dry_run)` now raises a clear `RuntimeError` on the LIVE path when no store path is derivable (instead of silently using `~/.brutex/lake`); the dry-run path still tolerates a missing store (synthetic fetcher never touches disk). `app.py` is coverage-excluded; the new guard is `# pragma: no cover` (live-only).
    - **N1 (NIT) — surface `no_data`/`skipped`.** `TimeframeResult` gained `no_data`/`skipped`; `PullCandlesReport` gained `total_no_data`/`total_skipped` (summed from `report.outcomes[...]`) + both in `to_dict`. `universe.html` now shows "no data returned" (yellow ∅) instead of a green ✓ when `total_completed == 0`. Locked by `test_no_data_and_skipped_propagate` (an `_EmptyFetcher` drives the NO_DATA branch) + extended `test_totals_and_to_dict`.
    - **Re-verification:** ruff check + `ruff format --check` clean; mypy --strict clean (3 dashboard files); pyright 0/0; interrogate 100% docstrings; `coverage run -m pytest` on the two dashboard test modules → 33 passed, both `pull_controller.py` + `universe_query.py` **100.00% line + branch**; full unit suite 2083 passed, 2 skipped (route-smoke test still gated on `[ui]`/FastAPI). The live Groww pull path remains unvalidatable offline (network blocked + no TOTP creds) — `_build_live_pull_fetcher` + the M1 live guard stay `# pragma: no cover`, exactly as before.

    **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):**
    - ✅ REAL now (offline): classification, summary, listing, and the dry-run pull (`pull_candles`, for spot OR a future/option contract symbol) all run + are unit-tested with no creds/network; the `/universe` page renders against the real endpoints and the synthetic pull animates end-to-end for the spot button, the future row, and the CE/PE cells (all share the same `runPull` + endpoint).
    - 🟡 LIVE-ON-UNBLOCK: the live Groww candle pull (`_build_live_pull_fetcher`) is unvalidatable here — network is blocked and Groww TOTP creds are absent — so it ships `# pragma: no cover`, exactly like the CLI's live path. When creds + network are present the `POST /pull` route flips to live automatically. The future/option pull paths share this same live spine, so they are equally proven only offline (dry-run) here.
    - F&O-ONLY scope (Golden Rule #3): the explorer browses + pulls into the LAKE only (DEC-P0-063). The **BRUTE-FORCE ENGINE is UNCHANGED** — still NIFTY/BANKNIFTY/SENSEX; pulling a stock's spot puts it in the lake in `REFERENCE_ONLY`, ready for a separately-authorised future engine expansion (not authorised here).
    - DEFERRED: the `/universe` HTML route + its JS cannot be unit-covered (app.py is coverage-excluded per `[tool.coverage.run].omit`; a route-registration smoke test is gated on the `[ui]` extra and skips when FastAPI is absent). Live drift/VIX-presence probes remain Phase 0b gates (CLAUDE.md §6), untouched here.

    **Verification:** ruff check + format clean; mypy --strict clean (114 files); pyright clean on the new files (0 errors, 0 warnings); 23 module tests (15 `universe_query` + 8 `pull_controller`) + 2 gated route-smoke tests. New modules 100% line + branch (interrogate 100% docstrings). **Post-build refinements re-verified:** ruff + `ruff format --check` clean; mypy --strict clean (114 files); pyright 0/0 on the 3 dashboard files; `coverage run -m pytest` on the two dashboard test modules → 23 passed, both modules 100.00% line + branch (`coverage run` used not `pytest --cov` due to the sandbox's `fastbloom_rs` double-init quirk). The frontend JS in `universe.html` + the `app.py` routes remain coverage-excluded (app.py per `[tool.coverage.run].omit`; the route-smoke test stays gated on `[ui]` and skips without FastAPI).

    **Decision ID:** DEC-UNIVERSE-DASHBOARD-001. **Builds on:** DEC-P0-063 (entry #200, LAKE universe-expansion scope authority) · DEC-FETCH-UNIVERSE-001 (entry #299, the `run_universe_ingest` + `brutex fetch --universe` surface this browses) · the dashboard design spec (`docs/TRACK1_DASHBOARD_DESIGN.md` v28-hybrid palette + the approved `docs/dashboard/mockups/universe-explorer-v1.html`).

305. **DEC-METRICS-001 — `brutex/metrics` per-result trading metrics implemented (was an 18-line stub)** (2026-05-29, Track 1 engine-completion work). Turns the placeholder `brutex/metrics/__init__.py` into a real, tested module that computes the per-trial metrics defined in `docs/TRACK1_BACKTEST_SPEC.md` §"Result Row Schema (Every Single Trial — 30+ Metrics)" (spec lines 512-579). 

    **What landed:** `brutex/metrics/types.py` (`ClosedTrade` — the canonical round-trip record with int64-paisa `pnl`/`mfe`/`mae` + holding span + tz-aware entry/exit + `Side`; `EquityCurve` — scalar int64-paisa equity samples + capital base + span). `brutex/metrics/compute.py` (`compute_metrics()` + `ResultMetrics` value object + numerically-stable Welford/Pébay-2008 streaming-moment accumulator). `brutex/metrics/__init__.py` re-exports the public surface. Tests: `tests/unit/test_metrics_compute.py` (46 known-value + edge-case tests) + `tests/property/test_metrics_properties.py` (6 hypothesis invariants: profit-factor sign law, drawdown sign, Sharpe sign-matches-mean, Sharpe scale-invariance, accounting identity, no-NaN/inf). `brutex/metrics` is **100% line + 100% branch**; mypy --strict + pyright + ruff + bandit all clean; full suite 2372 passed / 46 skipped / 1 xfail (no regressions).

    **Metrics implemented (32), each cited to the spec line:** Return (5) `net_pnl_paisa`/`total_return_pct`/`cagr_pct`/`gross_profit_paisa`/`gross_loss_paisa` (lines 532-537); Risk-adjusted (6) `sharpe`/`sortino`/`calmar`/`mar`/`information`/`omega` (lines 539-545); Drawdown (8) `max_drawdown_pct`/`_paisa`/`_duration_bars`/`recovery_factor`/`ulcer_index`/`largest_losing_trade_paisa`/`max_consecutive_losses`/`_wins` (lines 547-555); Trade quality (11) counts + `win_rate`/`profit_factor`/`avg_win`/`avg_loss`/`win_loss_ratio`/`expectancy_paisa`/`avg_mfe_paisa`/`avg_mae_paisa` (lines 557-568); Timing (3) `avg_holding_time_bars`/`max_holding_time_bars`/`time_in_market_pct` (lines 570-573); Distribution (4) `tail_ratio`/`skewness`/`kurtosis`/`returns_std_dev` (lines 575-579). The spec gives explicit ratio formulas for calmar/mar (=CAGR/MaxDD), profit_factor (=gross_profit/gross_loss), recovery_factor (=net profit/MDD), tail_ratio (=p95/|p5|), omega (at 0% threshold), information (excess vs buy-and-hold), expectancy (money per trade), ulcer (time-weighted DD). Where the spec only NAMES a metric (Sharpe, Sortino, skewness, kurtosis) the textbook definition is used with the convention documented on the field.

    **Precision (DEC-MONEY-2DP-001, money-only):** money fields stay int64 paisa; ratios / percentages / statistical moments keep full float precision and are NEVER rounded — `brutex.money` is deliberately NOT imported. Undefined ratios (zero trades, no losers, zero-variance returns, single trade) return `None` (not `inf`/`NaN`) to honor the "No NaN/inf in result rows" gate (spec line 746).

    **Conventions chosen where the spec is SILENT (truthful disclosure):** (a) **risk-free rate = 0** — Track 1 is signal-only research and the spec's omega is "at 0% threshold"; (b) **Sharpe/Sortino are per-trade-return ratios with NO annualisation multiplier** — the spec lists `cagr_pct` separately as the annualised figure and does not pin a Sharpe annualisation factor or bar-period; within a per-(instrument,TF) leaderboard a constant multiplier does not change the z-score ranking (spec lines 617-618), so omitting it is ranking-neutral; (c) **population moments** (divide by n) for variance/skew/kurtosis (excess kurtosis, normal baseline subtracted); (d) **`information_ratio`** = excess total return (vs an optional `benchmark_return_pct`, default 0) ÷ per-trade-return std; (e) **`max_drawdown_pct` is NOT clamped at 100%** — a >100% figure is real, gate-relevant information when realised losses drive equity below the prior peak by more than the peak (the `max_drawdown_pct > 30%` rejection gate, spec line 597); a hypothesis test discovered and pinned this. **`mar_ratio`** is computed identically to `calmar_ratio` (CAGR/MaxDD%); the schema lists both as distinct columns (line 542-543, "mar = Calmar alternative") so both are emitted. **`ClosedTrade` is first-homed in `brutex/metrics`** (Golden Rule #11 COMMON): the execution layer emits `Fill` + `EquityPoint` but no round-trip aggregation existed; a future trade-builder must import this type, not redefine it.

    **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10) — DEFERRED, NOT implemented:** the six Stage-4 statistical-validity metrics — `deflated_sharpe_ratio`, `pbo_probability`, `cpcv_pass_rate`, `wfo_pass_rate`, `lockbox_sharpe`, `lockbox_mdd_pct` (spec lines 581-587, which the spec itself marks "Stage 4 only, NULL before then"). These require the cross-validation / walk-forward / lockbox filter cascade (DEC-EXTREME-001 entry #202) and are out of scope for this per-result module. Likewise the **composite ranking score** (spec lines 602-615) and the **6 hard rejection gates** (spec lines 589-600) are Stage-4 concerns and are NOT in this PR — they consume these per-result metrics and belong with the filter-cascade work. No metric formula was invented (Golden Rule #1): every implemented metric is traceable to a spec line or an inline spec formula, and every spec-named-but-unpinned convention is disclosed above.

    **Z+ hostile-review fixes (PR #179, pre-merge):** an independent adversarial reviewer verified the Pébay m2/m3/m4 recurrence against an exact-`Fraction` oracle (correct) and probed edge cases. It caught **one BLOCKER**: `cagr_pct = growth ** (1/span_years)` raised an uncaught `OverflowError` (and could return astronomically-large-finite garbage) for tiny spans (a sub-day backtest), violating the spec line-746 no-inf/NaN gate. Fixed: the exponentiation is now wrapped (`except OverflowError → inf`) and clamped to a finite `_CAGR_CAP_PCT = 1e9` (the Stage-4 z-score clamp [-3,+3] absorbs the magnitude); regression test `test_cagr_tiny_span_stays_finite_and_capped`. Also fixed (MEDIUM) the `information_ratio` horizon mismatch — now `(mean per-trade return − per-trade share of buy-and-hold) / per-trade std`, comparable across differing trade counts (collapses to Sharpe at zero benchmark); and removed a dead double-guard on `win_loss_ratio` (LOW). The reviewer confirmed tests are non-vacuous (planted-bug probes failed the suite) and 37/37 schema field names match the spec exactly.

    **Decision ID:** DEC-METRICS-001. **Builds on:** `TRACK1_BACKTEST_SPEC.md` §"Result Row Schema" (the metric definitions) · DEC-EXTREME-001 (#202, the Stage-4 filter pipeline that owns the 6 deferred metrics) · DEC-MONEY-2DP-001 (money-display-only precision rule) · `brutex/execution/types.py` (the `Fill`/`EquityPoint` surface this module consumes alongside `ClosedTrade`) · Golden Rules #1/#10/#11.

304. **DEC-OPS-AUTOMATION-001 — standing rule: authentication + every process fully automated, zero operator clicks/day** (operator directive 2026-05-29 "always authentication should be fully automated … all these process should be completely comprehensively automated"). Own ledger header for the standing rule first stated in entry #300 §(c) (which bundled three decisions under one header; this gives the rule its own validator-discoverable entry). Re-affirms TOTP-only auth (CLAUDE.md §6: the API-Key+Secret flow is REFUSED because it needs daily manual Cloud-Console approval; the TOTP code is self-minted via `pyotp` and the access token auto-`reauth`-es on rollover with a clock-skew retry — zero manual steps, including across the vendor's "keys reset daily at 6 AM"). Extends Golden Rule #11 AUTOMATED to "every process, every run, zero operator clicks per day". Full detail + honest envelope in entry #300 §(c). **Decision ID:** DEC-OPS-AUTOMATION-001. **Builds on:** entry #300 (the rule bundle) · CLAUDE.md §6 (TOTP auth) · Golden Rule #11.

303. **DEC-INSTRUMENT-TESTFILTER-001 — drop vendor internal test instruments from the lake** (operator finding 2026-05-29, from the real `instrument.csv` export). Own ledger header for the filter first stated in entry #300 §(b). The vendor seeds the instrument master with non-tradable test rows — e.g. `031NSETEST36DECFUT` / `061NSETEST…` (underlying `…NSETEST`, expiry 2036-11-27, strike `-0.01`, freeze_quantity 100001). `brutex/adapters/groww/instruments.py::_is_test_instrument_row` filters any row whose identifying columns contain `NSETEST` (case-insensitive substring) OR whose `strike_price` is negative, counted into `InstrumentRefreshResult.filtered`. 2 unit tests; `instruments.py` stays 100% line+branch. Full detail in entry #300 §(b). **Decision ID:** DEC-INSTRUMENT-TESTFILTER-001. **Builds on:** entry #300 (the rule bundle) · DEC-FETCH-UNIVERSE-001 (#299, the universe ingest it guards) · DEC-P0-063 (#200, LAKE scope).

302. **DEC-RUN-PREFLIGHT-001 — `make run` preflight that internally verifies every host tool + auto-installs everything project-owned** (operator directive 2026-05-29 "when i run the application no manual configuration no manual check nothing … every language dependencies tools should be internally verified configured and i will just click run application alone … assure me the guarantee"). Adds `scripts/run/preflight.sh`, run as the first step of the `make run` target (and a standalone `make preflight`). It verifies the three tools the bootstrapper itself depends on — `python3.12`, `make`, `cargo` — prints a ✅/❌ table, and on a miss exits non-zero with the EXACT OS-specific one-line install command (brew on Darwin, apt on Linux, rustup for cargo) instead of a cryptic downstream crash. Everything the PROJECT owns (venv, all Python libs via `.[data,ui]`, the `fno_math` Rust kernel) is already auto-installed by `make run`; preflight makes the host-prereq layer self-checking too. Verified locally: happy path (all three present) exits 0; a missing interpreter exits 1 with the apt fix line. **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #1 + #10) — the guarantee, stated truthfully:** one click installs/verifies/configures everything *except* the three base tools, because a process cannot install the interpreter and `make` that it is itself written in (chicken-and-egg). Preflight NEVER auto-runs `curl | sh` or `sudo` (settings deny-list) — installing a system tool stays an explicit operator-consented step. So the honest guarantee is: **on any machine with python3.12 + make + cargo present, one click does literally everything else with zero manual steps; on a bare machine, preflight tells you the single command to run first.** No "100% zero-setup on bare OS" claim is made — that would be a hallucination. **Decision ID:** DEC-RUN-PREFLIGHT-001. **Builds on:** DEC-CLONE-RUN-002 (#297, the turnkey `make run`) · DEC-IDE-RUNCONFIG-001 (#301, the one-click button that triggers it).

301. **DEC-IDE-RUNCONFIG-001 — one-click IntelliJ/PyCharm "brutex (one-click · make run)" run configuration** (operator directive 2026-05-29 "inside intellij … just run brutex … where when I click that everything will be internally fully taken care of"). The repo already shipped `.idea/runConfigurations/BruteX.xml`, but that is a plain `PythonConfigurationType` running `brutex run` — it assumes the interpreter, deps, and `fno_math` kernel are already set up, so on a fresh clone the click fails (`ModuleNotFoundError: fno_math`, or no interpreter). This entry adds `.idea/runConfigurations/brutex_one_click.xml`, a `ShConfigurationType` (Shell Script) config that runs `make run` in a terminal tab from `$PROJECT_DIR$` — the turnkey path (DEC-CLONE-RUN-002, #297): create `.venv` → install `.[data,ui]` → build the `fno_math` Rust kernel if absent → launch the dashboard + browser. Idempotent re-runs skip done work. `.gitignore` already un-ignores `.idea/runConfigurations/` so the config is VCS-shared with every clone. README §Setup documents the dropdown + prerequisites. **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** the machine still needs `python3.12` + `make` + `cargo` (one-time kernel build) on PATH — these are host prerequisites the run config cannot install; everything downstream is automatic. Shell Script run configs need the bundled Shell Script plugin (present in IDEA Community/Ultimate + PyCharm). **Decision ID:** DEC-IDE-RUNCONFIG-001. **Builds on:** DEC-CLONE-RUN-002 (entry #297, the `make run` turnkey this exposes as a button).

300. **DEC-MONEY-2DP-001 + DEC-INSTRUMENT-TESTFILTER-001 + DEC-OPS-AUTOMATION-001 — operator rule bundle: 2-decimal money display, Groww test-instrument filter, fully-automated auth** (operator directive 2026-05-29 "always authentication should be fully automated … all these process should be completely comprehensively automated … after decimal points only two digits are allowed always maximum"). Three rules landed together off the same directive:

    **(a) DEC-MONEY-2DP-001 — money is displayed with ≤ 2 decimal places, money-only.** New single-source-of-truth module `brutex/money.py` (`round_money`, `paisa_to_rupees`, `format_inr`) — the ONLY place paisa→rupee display happens. Track 1 stores prices as int64 paisa (CLAUDE.md §6); this rounds (ROUND_HALF_UP) **at the display boundary only**. **Scope guard (Golden Rule #10):** the 2-decimal rule applies to MONEY ONLY. Statistical metrics (Sharpe, Deflated Sharpe, p-values, CPCV scores) keep FULL precision — rounding them would corrupt strategy ranking. Internal storage stays int64 paisa. Operator chose "Money/prices only" when asked (2026-05-29). 14 unit tests, 100% line+branch coverage, mypy strict + pyright clean. CLAUDE.md §4 updated with the rule.

    **(b) DEC-INSTRUMENT-TESTFILTER-001 — drop Groww internal test instruments from the lake.** The real instrument master (operator export 2026-05-29) seeds non-tradable test rows — e.g. `031NSETEST36DECFUT` / `061NSETEST…` (underlying `…NSETEST`, expiry 2036-11-27, strike `-0.01`, freeze_quantity 100001). `instruments.py::_is_test_instrument_row` now filters any row whose identifying columns contain `NSETEST` (case-insensitive substring) OR whose `strike_price` is negative, counted into `InstrumentRefreshResult.filtered`. 2 new tests; `instruments.py` remains 100% line+branch.

    **(c) DEC-OPS-AUTOMATION-001 — auth + processes fully automated, standing rule.** Re-affirms TOTP-only auth (CLAUDE.md §6: API-Key+Secret REFUSED — needs daily manual Cloud-Console approval; TOTP self-minted via `pyotp` + auto-`reauth` on token rollover with clock-skew retry — zero manual steps, including across Groww's "keys reset daily at 6 AM"). Extends Golden Rule #11 AUTOMATED to "every process, every run, zero operator clicks per day".

    **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):**
    - ✅ REAL: money helper + test-instrument filter both land with 100% coverage, ruff/mypy/pyright green, validated in the venv.
    - 🟡 PARTIAL: `format_inr` is the canonical helper but is **not yet wired into every money display site** (the engine's P&L/cost output paths are not built yet — metrics modules are still incomplete). Wiring is a tracked follow-up; the rule + helper are the enforceable artifact today. Western thousands-grouping (`1,234.56`), not Indian lakh/crore grouping — a cosmetic follow-up.
    - 🟡 PARTIAL: auto-reauth is threaded through the **candle** ingest (`run_ingest(reauth=...)`) but the live `fetch --universe` loop (`_run_live_universe_ingest`, pragma-no-cover) currently uses a single session without mid-loop reauth threading. Tracked follow-up to mirror the candle path; unvalidatable here (Groww blocked).
    - ❌ NOT DONE: live Groww paths remain unvalidatable in this environment (network 403 + no creds).

    **Decision ID:** DEC-MONEY-2DP-001 · DEC-INSTRUMENT-TESTFILTER-001 · DEC-OPS-AUTOMATION-001. **Builds on:** DEC-FETCH-UNIVERSE-001 (entry #299, the universe ingest these refine) · CLAUDE.md §6 (paisa storage + TOTP auth) · Golden Rule #10 (honest envelope) + #11 (AUTOMATED).

299. **DEC-FETCH-UNIVERSE-001 — `brutex fetch --universe` turnkey: ingest instrument master + expiries + contracts into the lake BEFORE index candles** (operator directive 2026-05-29 "pull entire instruments + all expiries + all contracts + current-year NIFTY/BANKNIFTY/SENSEX all timeframes in one command"). Adds a `--universe` boolean flag to the existing `brutex fetch` CLI command. When set, `cmd_fetch` first runs the LAKE-surface discovery pass — instrument master (Surface B) → expiries (Surface C) → contracts (Surface D) — and only then the existing 3-index candle ingest. The pass is a new pure-orchestration core, `brutex/adapters/groww/universe_ingest.py::run_universe_ingest`, that REUSES the existing adapter primitives (`InstrumentMasterFetcher`, `ExpiryFetcher`, `ContractFetcher`) with their already-injected stores — no copy-paste (Golden Rule #11 COMMON). It refreshes the master once, then for every `(underlying, year)` discovers expiries and for every discovered expiry discovers contracts, aggregating per-surface counts into `UniverseIngestReport`.

    - **Offline/dry-run:** `--universe --dry-run` wires a synthetic instrument CSV (`SYNTHETIC_INSTRUMENT_CSV`) through the REAL `InstrumentMasterFetcher` parse path plus synthetic expiry/contract sources, so the whole orchestration runs with NO network and NO Groww creds.
    - **Live wiring:** `_run_live_universe_ingest` (`# pragma: no cover` — needs live Groww SDK + creds + DuckDB) builds the real auth + SDK fetchers, mirroring the existing `_build_live_fetcher` pattern.

    **Scope (Golden Rule #3):** instruments/expiries/contracts ingest is the LAKE surface authorised by **DEC-P0-063** (entry #200). The BRUTE-FORCE ENGINE is UNCHANGED — still the 3 indices (NIFTY/BANKNIFTY/SENSEX); the discovered F&O universe sits in the lake in `REFERENCE_ONLY`. No engine scope expansion.

    **Tests:** `tests/unit/test_groww_universe_ingest.py` (3 tests) covers the orchestration core with fakes — aggregation across surfaces, empty-underlyings path, and the synthetic-CSV header/parse contract — at 100% line + 100% branch on `universe_ingest.py`. `tests/unit/test_cli_run.py` extended with 3 tests for `--universe` arg parsing + `main` dispatch threading `universe=args.universe` into `cmd_fetch`. The module imports without the `fno_math` Rust kernel (it uses `TYPE_CHECKING` for the heavy result types), so the new tests collect even on an unbuilt environment.

    **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):**
    - ✅ REAL: `--universe` flag + dispatch + pure orchestration core, all unit-tested offline; `universe_ingest.py` at 100% line+branch; global coverage gate stays ≥ 90% (97.03% locally on the collectable subset).
    - 🟡 PARTIAL: the end-to-end `--universe --dry-run` CLI path could NOT be executed in the authoring container — the DuckDB `InstrumentStore` transitively imports the unbuilt `fno_math` kernel; the dry-run helper + synthetic sources are therefore `# pragma: no cover` (same rationale as the pre-existing `test_groww_contracts`/`test_groww_expiries` collection skips). The orchestration logic they drive IS fully covered via the fake-injected core.
    - ❌ NOT DONE: the LIVE Groww path is unvalidatable here (Groww hosts blocked + no creds + `growwapi` not installed) — `# pragma: no cover`, validated only once the operator clears the network/creds walls per `docs/runbooks/data-fetch-runday.md` §1.

    **Decision ID:** DEC-FETCH-UNIVERSE-001. **Builds on:** DEC-P0-063 (entry #200, LAKE universe-expansion scope authority) · DEC-GROWW-ADAPTER-DESIGN-001 (Surfaces B/C/D) · DEC-GROWW-INGEST-001 (the `brutex fetch` candle command this extends).

298. **DEC-SESSION-HOOK-LIVESTATE-001 — SessionStart hook now auto-surfaces live pipeline / audit / alerting state on every Claude session** (operator directive 2026-05-29 "every new or existing Claude code session ... should automatically do everything as an automated process always ... access logs queries dbs ... entirely accessible as an automated process"). Extends DEC-SESSION-START-HOOK-001 (entry #237): the prior hook printed *repo* state (branch, last-5 decisions, CLAUDE.md freshness, venv/fno_math presence) but said nothing about what the **system** is doing right now. This entry adds an **"Automation & live state"** section to `.claude/hooks/session-start.sh` so each session inherits the live operational surface without the operator running anything by hand:

    - **Pipeline status** — runs `brutex status` (reads the runtime file; cheap; always run when a `.venv/` python is present, else prints a `make setup` hint).
    - **Audit chain** — counts JSONL logs in `${BRUTEX_AUDIT_DIR:-~/.brutex/audit}` and points at `brutex doctor` for full hash-chain verification (cheap directory probe; always run).
    - **Alerting (Telegram)** — reports whether alerts are armed by checking `BRUTEX_TELEGRAM_ENABLED` + `BRUTEX_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN` **presence only** (the secret value is never printed).
    - **Doctor (full health check)** — opt-in via `BRUTEX_SESSION_HOOK_DOCTOR=1`; runs `brutex doctor` and tails the summary. Off by default so session startup stays fast.
    - **Automation commands** — one-line menu (`make run · brutex status · brutex doctor · brutex monitor · brutex dashboard`).

    **Safety contract (unchanged from DEC-SESSION-START-HOOK-001):** notification-only, exit 0 always, all output → stderr (stdout stays clean for downstream pipes), no `set -e`. Every probe is time-boxed (`timeout`/`gtimeout` 8s, cross-platform for the macOS M4 Pro envelope where `timeout` is absent) and `|| true`-guarded, so a missing venv / CLI / log directory degrades to a one-line skip rather than a hard failure. Section labels are printed unconditionally so the output contract holds regardless of environment.

    **Tests:** `tests/unit/test_session_start_hook.py::TestOutputContent` extended with 6 new required tokens (`Automation & live state`, `DEC-SESSION-HOOK-LIVESTATE-001`, `Pipeline status:`, `Audit chain:`, `Alerting (Telegram):`, `Automation commands:`). Existing exit-code / stdout-clean / descending-decisions contracts unchanged. Verified locally: `bash -n` clean; manual run → exit 0, stdout 0 bytes, all 6 tokens present, graceful skips when venv/logs absent; `ruff check` on the test file GREEN.

    **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):**
    - ✅ REAL: cheap probes (status read, audit-dir count, alerting-presence) run on every session today; section labels always present; contract test extended.
    - 🟡 PARTIAL: the heavy full health check (`brutex doctor`) is opt-in (`BRUTEX_SESSION_HOOK_DOCTOR=1`), not always-on, to keep startup latency low — a deliberate honest trade, not always-running "everything".
    - 📋 ROADMAP: auto-emitting the session summary to Telegram (vs. stderr only) and a daily cron wrapper for `brutex doctor` remain follow-ups (the alerting wiring exists per DEC-TELEGRAM-WIRING-001 but is not invoked from the hook).
    - ❌ NOT DONE: no AWS / remote-DB access — out of Track 1 scope (operator chose "keep local-only" 2026-05-29); pytest was not run locally (no venv in the authoring container) — CI is the gate.

    **Decision ID:** DEC-SESSION-HOOK-LIVESTATE-001. **Builds on:** DEC-SESSION-START-HOOK-001 (entry #237, the base hook) · DEC-DOCTOR-001 (`brutex doctor` CLI) · DEC-MONITOR-LOOP-001 (`brutex monitor`) · DEC-TELEGRAM-WIRING-001 (alerting transport). **Scope note (Golden Rule #3):** surfacing-only — no new engine behaviour, no scope expansion.

297. **DEC-CLONE-RUN-002 — turnkey `make run`/`make setup`: venv + all libs + `fno_math` kernel auto-built; `ui` extra added to bootstrap** (operator directive 2026-05-28 "fully completely comprehensively automated — just run application and everything should be entirely visible ... libs and all other needed everything should be automatically included"). *(Renumbered #279 → #297 on 2026-05-28 when this entry was reconciled into the #174 branch: `main`'s DEC-CLONE-RUN-002 had taken #279, which collided with this branch's DEC-RESAMPLE-ANCHOR-001 (#279); re-seated as the next free number, newest-first.)* Extends DEC-CLONE-RUN-001 (entry #278): the prior `make run` installed `.[data,ui]` then launched the dashboard, but it (a) used whatever Python happened to be active (no venv), and (b) never built the maturin `fno_math` Rust kernel — so any import of `brutex.strategies.combo` (top-level `import fno_math` at `combo.py:64`) crashed `ModuleNotFoundError` on a clean clone. Separately, `scripts/setup/bootstrap.sh` installed `[dev,data,groww]` but **omitted `ui`**, so a post-bootstrap `python -m brutex dashboard` failed for missing fastapi/uvicorn.

    **What this fixes:**

    - **`make run` is now self-contained + idempotent** — creates `.venv/` (if absent), upgrades pip, installs `.[data,ui]`, then builds the `fno_math` kernel via `maturin develop --release` *only when it is not importable* (needs `cargo`), then launches the one-click `brutex run`. Re-running skips work already done.
    - **`make setup`** added — turnkey full dev env; delegates to `scripts/setup/bootstrap.sh` (single source of truth per CLAUDE.md §11 COMMON — no recipe copy-paste).
    - **`bootstrap.sh` now installs `.[dev,data,ui,groww]`** (was missing `ui`).
    - **README §Setup** updated to describe the venv + kernel-build flow honestly (Golden Rule #1).

    **Verification (fresh `.venv`, py3.12, `cargo` 1.94):** `pip install -e .[data,ui]` pulled fastapi 0.136 / uvicorn 0.48 / polars 1.41 / duckdb 1.5 / pyarrow 24; `maturin develop --release` built `fno_math` (~29 s); `import fno_math` + the previously-crashing `import brutex.strategies.combo` both succeed; `make run` served `GET /` → 200 (214 515 B), `/api/v1/state` → 200, `/metrics` → 200, `/api/v1/instruments` → 503 (no lake, expected).

    **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** build/run-automation only — no engine scope, no new CI gate, no coverage-gated module touched. The kernel build needs a working `cargo` toolchain (present in CI + the reference M4 Pro). `make run` launches the *placeholder* dashboard — real numbers need the lake + engine (Phase 2).
