358. **DEC-PULL-PROGRESS-LIVE-001 — the live feed lands: background pull job + `/pull-progress` polling route + dashboard JS that ticks the counters window-by-window** (operator 2026-06-01: "real time live dashboard where even millions of users should be always easily attracted and impressed"). **Three pieces:** **(1) `brutex/dashboard/pull_jobs.py::BackgroundPullJob`** — runs a pull off the request thread and drives a `PullProgress` (DEC-PULL-PROGRESS-TRACKER-001), so the HTTP route returns at once and the page polls the O(1) snapshot. **Single-flight** under a `threading.Lock` (a second `start` while running is a no-op → `False`, so two universe pulls never race the same lake — CLAUDE.md §2 Rule 7); the injected work receives the drivers' positional `on_progress` hook (#357) adapted onto the tracker, and **any** worker exception is captured as the job's `error` state (never crashes the server thread). The spawner is injected → 100% line+branch unit-tested synchronously (+ a real-daemon-thread end-to-end test) with a strict per-module CI gate. **(2) The `/api/v1/universe/pull-all` + `/pull-options` routes now LAUNCH the job** (return `{"started": …}` immediately) and a new **`GET /api/v1/universe/pull-progress`** returns `BackgroundPullJob.snapshot().to_dict()` (state / total / completed / failed / bars / percent / current). **(3) The `/universe` JS** POSTs to start, then polls `/pull-progress` every 600 ms and ticks the counter + percentage + live stat row until `done`/`error` — the visible real-time feed. **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** the routes + JS are the coverage-omitted live-wiring layer (`app.py` + template, exercised manually like the existing `/pull` route); the **tested** core is `BackgroundPullJob` + `PullProgress`. The page polls (600 ms) rather than true Server-Sent-Events streaming — a poll is simpler + proxy-safe and already gives a smooth per-window tick; an SSE upgrade is an optional future refinement, not a gap in the feed. The live panel shows the **aggregate** counters (units done / total / failed / bars), not the per-symbol table (browse `/db` for rows). **Builds on:** DEC-PULL-PROGRESS-WIRING-001 (#357) · DEC-PULL-PROGRESS-TRACKER-001 (#356) · DEC-UNIVERSE-DASHBOARD-BUTTONS-001 (#355) · DEC-UNIVERSE-PULL-ROUTES-001 (#354) · CLAUDE.md §2 Rule 5 (O(1)) / Rule 7 (idempotence) / §4 (no global mutable state) / §11 COMMON.

359. **DEC-OPTION-FETCH-COVERAGE-001 — the options fetch-coverage ledger: a re-pull skips already-pulled option windows so no Dhan hit is wasted** (operator 2026-06-01 standing decree: "not to waste the dhan hit limits … with tracking"; the options analog of the merged spot watermark DEC-FETCH-COVERAGE-001 #350). `ingest_rolling_option_series` now takes an optional `coverage: FetchCoverage` (the **same** COMMON ledger as spot — CLAUDE.md §11): each rolling-option window is keyed by an O(1) `WindowKey(market="dhan-option", security_id="<id>|<segment>", interval="OPT|<flag>|<code>|<strike>|<type>", start, end)` — the full series coordinate folded into one reusable key so two series never collide. A window already marked complete is **skipped before the vendor call** (`skipped_windows` counted; no wasted 5 r/s Dhan budget — `docs/dhan-ref/01`), and marked complete only after a clean fetch + append. Threaded through `run_dhan_options_ingest` (new `skipped_windows` aggregate on `OptionsUniverseReport`) and `pull_options`. **O(1)** per window (one hash probe + one add); when `coverage` is `None` (every current caller) the path is unchanged. The idempotent option sink stays the correctness backstop, so skipping is a pure quota/latency optimisation that can never lose or duplicate data (CLAUDE.md §2 Rule 7). **All three strict-gated modules stay 100% line+branch** (mark-then-skip re-pull tests added). **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** this ships the **skip wiring + tests**; persisting the options ledger across runs (DuckDB/lake-backed) + wiring a live ledger into the dashboard options pull are tracked follow-ups (same staging as the spot ledger). **Builds on:** DEC-FETCH-COVERAGE-001 (#350) · DEC-DHAN-OPTIONS-PULL-CONTROLLER-001 (#353) · DEC-DHAN-PULL-SCOPE-001 (#348) · CLAUDE.md §2 Rule 5 (O(1)) / Rule 6 (rate-limit) / Rule 7 (idempotence) / §11 COMMON.

360. **DEC-DHAN-CREDS-WIRING-001 — the credentials page is now Dhan AND actually enables live pulls (env-first, file-fallback)** (operator 2026-06-01, after a 3-agent deep-research trace of why "real time everything is failing" while dry-run looked fine). **Root cause (verified to file:line by all 3 agents):** the dashboard Credentials page saved **Groww** API-key + TOTP to `~/.brutex/credentials.json` (`{"groww": …}`), but every spot/options pull gated on `dhan_creds_present()` which read **only the env vars** `BRUTEX_DHAN_ACCESS_TOKEN`/`BRUTEX_DHAN_CLIENT_ID` and **never the saved file** — so setting creds in the UI did nothing and every pull silently forced `dry_run` (synthetic). **Fix:** `brutex/config/credentials.py` is rewritten as the **Dhan** file store (`DhanFileCredentials(access_token, client_id)`, section `{"dhan": …}`, env vars `BRUTEX_DHAN_*`), preserving the full security machinery verbatim (mode-0600 atomic write, O_NOFOLLOW + fstat perms check / TOCTOU refusal, masked preview). `brutex/adapters/dhan/auth.py::resolve_dhan_credentials` + `dhan_creds_present` now resolve **env-first, then the saved file**, so saving creds on the page (or `brutex auth set`) **flips the spot/options buttons to LIVE**. The credentials page (`credentials.html`), the `/api/v1/credentials` route + `CredentialsIn`, and the CLI `auth set/status/clear` are all retargeted to Access Token + Client ID. **No-hallucination note (Golden Rule #1):** a research agent claimed `adapter.py:120`'s `_EQ` segment for indices was a bug needing `IDX_I`; the Dhan docs (`docs/dhan-ref/09:77`) confirm indices use `NSE_EQ`/`BSE_EQ` + the `INDEX` instrument type — so that "fix" was REJECTED (it would have broken live index pulls). `config.credentials` + `adapters.dhan.auth` both stay **100% line+branch**. **Builds on:** DEC-CREDENTIALS-STORE-001 · DEC-VENDOR-PIVOT-001 · CLAUDE.md §6 (verified Dhan facts) / §11 COMMON.

361. **DEC-DHAN-FINALIZE-001 — the residual Groww adapter is deleted; BruteX is Dhan-only** (operator 2026-06-01: "still how groww related dashboard is still available … nothing is looking similar to dhan"). The DEC-DHAN-CREDS-WIRING-001 (#360) rename of the shared `config.credentials` API forced resolving the legacy Groww surface that still depended on it. Removed in full: the entire **`brutex/adapters/groww/`** package (15 modules) + its **20 test files**; the legacy Groww three-phase universe ingest in `cli.py` (`_run_dry_run_universe_ingest`, `_run_live_universe_ingest`, `_build_universe_fetchers`, `_build_remint_guard`, `_run_expiries_phase`, `_run_contracts_phase`, the module-level `RemintGuard` import, and `fetch --universe`'s Groww path → now a retired-notice); the dashboard's Groww-backed routes (`/api/v1/universe/fetch-expiries`, `/fetch-contracts`, `/phase-status`, `/api/v1/coverage/repull`) + their phase/repull helpers; and the now-dead UI (the two-phase buttons in `universe.html`, the "Re-pull gaps" button in `coverage.html`). The new Dhan 8-button pull (spot + options, #353/#354/#355/#358) fully supersedes the two-phase flow. **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** the `groww` optional-dependency extra + `growwapi`/`pyotp` deps + their mypy overrides in `pyproject.toml` / `Makefile` are now **unused but still present** — nothing imports `growwapi`, the CI groww per-module gates are `compgen`-guarded (self-heal to "not present"), and the install lines just pull an unused wheel; their removal is a tracked **follow-up** (kept out of this PR to bound the blast radius). A handful of Sphinx docstring cross-references to `brutex.adapters.groww.*` remain (cosmetic, non-importing) and are swept in the same follow-up. Type-check (`mypy --strict` + `pyright`), lint, interrogate (100%), vulture, jscpd all green tree-wide after the deletion. **Builds on:** DEC-DHAN-CREDS-WIRING-001 (#360) · DEC-VENDOR-PIVOT-001 · DEC-UNIVERSE-DASHBOARD-BUTTONS-001 (#355) · CLAUDE.md §3 (scope) / §11 COMMON.

362. **DEC-DHAN-INSTRUMENT-CLASSIFY-001 — classify off `INSTRUMENT`, not `INSTRUMENT_TYPE` (bug #1 of the "120 indices / 1 stock" pair)** (operator 2026-06-01: "it is showing me 120 indices and only one stocks motherfucker why … what is the issue with indices finding and loading and even stocks loading", + "already we have implemented the precise instruments functionality in this tickvault … fetch and use the same code from here"). A deep-research agent **decoded the operator's real `api-scrip-master-detailed`** (219,082 data rows) to ground the fix — no assumption, no hallucination (Golden Rule #1). The detailed CSV carries TWO different vocabularies: **`INSTRUMENT`** = the long codes `INDEX / EQUITY / OPTSTK / OPTIDX / FUTSTK / FUTIDX / …` (ground-truth distinct: `OPTSTK` 105,194 · `OPTFUT` 48,276 · `OPTCUR` 23,616 · `EQUITY` 22,649 · `OPTIDX` 17,422 · `FUTSTK` 1,270 · `INDEX` 194 · …), while **`INSTRUMENT_TYPE`** = short *security-class* codes `OP / ES / FUT / DEB / DBT / CB / ETF / INDEX / …`. The parser was keying classification on `INSTRUMENT_TYPE`, so every option/future leg read as `OP`/`FUT` — matching **no** derivative vocabulary — the F&O-eligible set came out empty, and only the 194 `INDEX` rows (≈ the "120 indices" the operator saw) survived while ~0 stocks were admitted. **Fix:** `instruments.py` now classifies off `INSTRUMENT` (`_INDEX_TYPES`/`_EQUITY_TYPES`/`_FUTURE_TYPES`/`_OPTION_TYPES` are the long codes; `_REQUIRED_COLUMNS` requires `INSTRUMENT`), mirroring the proven **tickvault** parser the operator pointed to (`crates/core/src/instrument/csv_parser.rs` classifies off `INSTRUMENT`). Paired with DEC-DHAN-FNO-JOIN-KEY-001 (#363) in one PR. **Builds on:** DEC-DHAN-INSTRUMENTS-001 (#345) · DEC-INSTRUMENT-UNIVERSE-FAST-001 (#160) · DEC-VENDOR-PIVOT-001 · CLAUDE.md §1 (universe) / §11 COMMON / Golden Rule #1.

363. **DEC-DHAN-FNO-JOIN-KEY-001 — the F&O-eligibility join keys on the equity's `UNDERLYING_SYMBOL` ticker, not its `SYMBOL_NAME` (bug #2 of the "120 indices / 1 stock" pair)** (operator 2026-06-01, same run as DEC-DHAN-INSTRUMENT-CLASSIFY-001 #362; same decoded ground-truth file, 219,082 rows). Even after #362, a second still-live bug remained: `select_universe_report` admitted an equity iff its **`SYMBOL_NAME`** was in the F&O-eligible set — but that set is built from derivative **`UNDERLYING_SYMBOL`** tickers, and in the real CSV an equity's `SYMBOL_NAME` is the **long company name** ("RELIANCE INDUSTRIES LTD", "ABB INDIA LIMITED") while its **`UNDERLYING_SYMBOL`** holds the **ticker** ("RELIANCE", "ABB") that matches the leg. Ground-truth match rates against the 229 F&O underlyings: equity `SYMBOL_NAME` = **19/229** (≈ "1 stock"); equity `UNDERLYING_SYMBOL` = **229/229**. **Fix:** the join now keys on the equity row's own `UNDERLYING_SYMBOL`, and an admitted spot's `trading_symbol` resolves to that ticker (so a stock's identity matches how its F&O legs reference it; indices + non-F&O equities have no `UNDERLYING_SYMBOL` → keep `SYMBOL_NAME`). Mirrors tickvault's `fno_underlying_extractor.rs` (resolves the stock-derivative underlying linkage, not the display name). **Tests:** `instruments.py` stays **100% line+branch** (121 stmts / 44 branches). The unit fixtures were rebuilt to the **real namespace split** — equity rows now carry a long `SYMBOL_NAME` + a ticker `UNDERLYING_SYMBOL` — so they actually exercise the join (a new `test_join_keys_on_underlying_ticker_not_symbol_name` proves a decoy `SYMBOL_NAME==ticker` row with no `UNDERLYING_SYMBOL` is **rejected** while the real long-name row is admitted with the ticker `trading_symbol`). The loader/refresh/economics/dashboard fixtures were updated in lockstep; full affected suite green (unit + duckdb-gated integration). **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** this fixes the **count + identity** of the admitted universe; the real index-row `SYMBOL_NAME` layout is unverified against the live CSV and is left as-is (indices keep `SYMBOL_NAME`), tracked as a Phase-0b probe. **Builds on:** DEC-DHAN-INSTRUMENT-CLASSIFY-001 (#362) · DEC-DHAN-INSTRUMENTS-001 (#345) · DEC-INSTRUMENT-UNIVERSE-FAST-001 (#160) · tickvault `csv_parser.rs` / `fno_underlying_extractor.rs` (operator-confirmed reference) · CLAUDE.md §1 / §11 COMMON / Golden Rule #1.

364. **DEC-DHAN-VENDOR-SYMBOL-UNIQUE-001 — `vendor_symbol` is the exchange-prefixed GLOBAL key; raw `SECURITY_ID` stays in `exchange_token` (fixes the live `duplicate key "13"` crash)** (operator 2026-06-01 `make run`: the "Load instruments" button returned HTTP 500 — `_duckdb.ConstraintException: PRIMARY KEY or UNIQUE constraint violation: duplicate key "13, 2026-06-01"` — and the startup autoload logged the same as `instrument_master.autoload_skipped`). **Root cause (verified against the candle-fetch code + tickvault's identity model — Golden Rule #1):** Dhan `SECURITY_ID` is unique **only within an exchange-segment**, NOT globally — an NSE row and a BSE row can share the same numeric id (e.g. `13`: NIFTY on NSE vs a BSE index). The Dhan parser set `vendor_symbol = SECURITY_ID`, and the instruments-table PK `(vendor_symbol, snapshot_date)` + the adapter's `{vendor_symbol: instrument}` O(1) index + `InstrumentSnapshot`'s dedup all treat `vendor_symbol` as globally unique → two `"13"` rows collided on insert. This **mirrors tickvault**, whose instrument identity is the `(exchange_segment, security_id)` pair (`ExchangeSegment` enum: `IDX_I`=0/`NSE_EQ`=1/`NSE_FNO`=2/`BSE_EQ`=4), **never the bare id** (operator: "go through the entire instrument functionality in depth … directly from tickvault"). It also restores the BruteX-wide contract that `vendor_symbol` is exchange-prefixed — `tests/conftest.py` (`"NSE-NIFTY"`), `test_models.py` (`"NSE-RELIANCE"`), and the existing `test_pull_symbol_is_exchange_prefixed` invariant all already assume it. **Fix (no schema migration — the parser was the lone violator):** `parse_instrument_row` now sets `vendor_symbol = f"{exchange}-{trading_symbol}"` (the global key) and keeps the raw `SECURITY_ID` in `exchange_token`; `DhanMarketAdapter.fetch_ohlcv` sends `str(instrument.exchange_token)` (the raw id the candle API needs) instead of the lookup `symbol`. A new regression test (`test_vendor_symbol_is_exchange_prefixed_global_key`) proves NSE-`13` and BSE-`13` get DISTINCT `vendor_symbol`s (`"NSE-NIFTY"` vs `"BSE-SENSEX"`) with the SAME `exchange_token` 13 → no PK collision; `instruments.py` + `adapter.py` stay **100% line+branch**, full suite green (2889 passed). **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** `vendor_symbol` embeds the **exchange** (sufficient for the observed cross-exchange collision; equity ids are 4–5 digits so an index-vs-equity clash within one exchange is not present in the real master). The fully tickvault-faithful key is the finer Dhan **exchange-segment** (`IDX_I`/`NSE_EQ`/`BSE_EQ` — indices on `IDX_I`); adopting that granularity (and whether the index candle fetch must use `IDX_I` rather than `_EQ`) is a tracked Phase-0b probe, not changed here. **Builds on:** DEC-DHAN-FNO-JOIN-KEY-001 (#363) · DEC-DHAN-INSTRUMENT-CLASSIFY-001 (#362) · DEC-DHAN-INSTRUMENTS-001 (#345) · DEC-INSTRUMENT-UNIVERSE-FAST-001 (#160) · tickvault `crates/common/src/types.rs` (`ExchangeSegment`) / `instrument_types.rs` (operator-confirmed reference) · CLAUDE.md §1 / §11 COMMON / Golden Rule #1.

365. **DEC-DHAN-UNIVERSE-DEDUP-001 — admit ONE canonical spot per global key (fixes the live `duplicate key "NSE-MOTHERSON"` crash)** (operator 2026-06-01 `make run`, immediately after #364 shipped: the autoload + "Load instruments" again 500'd — `_duckdb.ConstraintException: ... duplicate key "NSE-MOTHERSON, 2026-06-01"`). **Root cause (verified against the decoded real master + tickvault's resolver — Golden Rule #1):** the real Dhan master lists some F&O tickers under **more than one NSE equity row** — e.g. `SAMVARDHANA MOTHERSON INT` and `SAMVRDHNA MTHRSN INTL LTD`, both with `UNDERLYING_SYMBOL="MOTHERSON"` but different `SECURITY_ID`s. After #364 both resolve to the same exchange-prefixed `vendor_symbol` (`"NSE-MOTHERSON"`), so both were admitted and collided on the store PK. **Fix:** `select_universe_report` now **deduplicates to one canonical spot per `vendor_symbol`** via an O(1) dict — mirroring tickvault's `fno_underlying_extractor.rs` `UNDERLYING_SECURITY_ID` → NSE_EQ-spot resolution: the new helper `fno_underlying_security_ids` collects the canonical spot ids a derivative points to, and on a key clash the row whose `SECURITY_ID` is such a canonical id wins (else first-seen). Keeps the proven `UNDERLYING_SYMBOL` ticker eligibility (no stock-count regression) while guaranteeing a unique global key. New regression tests (`TestUniverseDedup`: canonical-preference + first-seen-fallback + `fno_underlying_security_ids`) keep `instruments.py` **100% line+branch** (135 stmts / 52 branches); affected suites green. **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** this guarantees no duplicate-`vendor_symbol` PK collision on the autoload/upsert path. A 3-agent deep audit vs tickvault (this session) flagged residual divergences NOT addressed here — BruteX still (a) has **no `*NSETEST` test-scrip exclusion**, (b) has **no NSE-only gate** on stock-derivative eligibility (tickvault excludes BSE single-stock F&O), and (c) uses a **ticker join** where tickvault uses the validated integer `UNDERLYING_SECURITY_ID` join — so BruteX admits a *superset* of tickvault's spots. These are tracked follow-ups; the strategic fix (let tickvault own instrument resolution and have BruteX import the resolved universe — single source of truth, ends the re-implementation drift) is proposed as a separate ADR. **Builds on:** DEC-DHAN-VENDOR-SYMBOL-UNIQUE-001 (#364) · DEC-DHAN-FNO-JOIN-KEY-001 (#363) · DEC-DHAN-INSTRUMENT-CLASSIFY-001 (#362) · DEC-INSTRUMENT-UNIVERSE-FAST-001 (#160) · tickvault `crates/core/src/instrument/fno_underlying_extractor.rs` (operator-confirmed reference) · CLAUDE.md §1 / §11 COMMON / Golden Rule #1.

366. **DEC-DHAN-UNIVERSE-TICKVAULT-PARITY-001 — NSE-only single-stock F&O + `*NSETEST` exclusion (close 2 of the 3 audited divergences)** (operator 2026-06-01: "stick to brutex itself dont need to touch tickvault" — port tickvault's logic faithfully, no coupling). Closes two residual divergences the #365 audit flagged: (a) the F&O-eligible stock set + admitted equity spots are now **NSE-only** (`_is_nse_stock_derivative` gates `FUTSTK`/`OPTSTK` to `EXCH_ID=NSE`; admission gates `EQUITY` to NSE) — BSE single-stock F&O is out of scope, mirroring tickvault's `STOCK_DERIVATIVE_PREFIXES` NSE_EQ-only gate; (b) Dhan placeholder **TEST scrips** (`*NSETEST`) are excluded from both eligibility and admission (`_is_test_scrip`, tickvault's `CSV_TEST_SYMBOL_MARKER`). **Indices are NOT gated to NSE** — the BSE **SENSEX** index stays admitted (operator: "sensex alone cant be skipped … for indices sensex"); the index allow-list is DEC-DHAN-INDEX-ALLOWLIST-001 (#367). 100% self-contained — tickvault is a read-only reference, nothing is wired to its repo/AWS (honors DEC-P0-026). `instruments.py` stays **100% line+branch**; new `TestTickvaultParity` (TEST-scrip exclusion, BSE stock-deriv not eligible, BSE equity not admitted, BSE SENSEX still admitted). **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** the 3rd audited divergence — ticker join vs tickvault's integer `UNDERLYING_SECURITY_ID` join — is deliberately kept as ticker-eligibility + canonical-spot dedup (DEC-DHAN-UNIVERSE-DEDUP-001), which equals tickvault's set when `UNDERLYING_SECURITY_ID` is populated and degrades safely (no 0-stock regression) when it isn't. **Builds on:** DEC-DHAN-UNIVERSE-DEDUP-001 (#365) · DEC-DHAN-VENDOR-SYMBOL-UNIQUE-001 (#364) · tickvault `fno_underlying_extractor.rs` (read-only reference) · CLAUDE.md §1 / §3 (scope) / §11 COMMON.

367. **DEC-DHAN-INDEX-ALLOWLIST-001 — admit ONLY the curated headline indices (30 NSE Index-tab symbols + BSE SENSEX), not all ~194 `INDEX` rows** (operator 2026-06-01: "whatever is shown here as indices i need only these indices alone … no need to have 100 plus indices … along with this one more index which is BSE SENSEX"; operator chose "Full Dhan Index-tab list"). The dashboard showed **120 INDEX** because `select_universe_report` admitted every `INDEX` row (NSE 119 + BSE 75 ≈ 194). It now admits only an explicit allow-list keyed on exact upper-cased `SYMBOL_NAME`: the **30 NSE Index-tab symbols** (`NIFTY`, `BANKNIFTY`, `FINNIFTY`, `MIDCPNIFTY`, `NIFTY NEXT 50`, `GIFTNIFTY`, `INDIA VIX`, `NIFTY 100/200/500`, `NIFTYMCAP50`, `NIFTY MIDCAP 150`, `NIFTY SMALLCAP 50/100/250`, `NIFTY MICROCAP250`, `NIFTY AUTO`, `NIFTYIT`, `NIFTY MEDIA/METAL/PHARMA/PSU BANK/PVT BANK/REALTY/FMCG/ENERGY/MNC/CONSUMPTION`, `NIFTYINFRA`, `NIFTY SERV SECTOR`) **+ BSE `SENSEX`** — the EXACT tokens verified 2026-06-01 against the decoded real master (abbreviated, not the web display names). Every other `INDEX` row is dropped (`_is_admitted_index`); edit the one `_ADMITTED_NSE_INDEX_SYMBOLS` set to add/remove. `instruments.py` **100% line+branch**; new `TestIndexAllowList` (obscure NSE index dropped, BSE non-SENSEX dropped, helper case-insensitive + non-NSE/BSE branch). **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** the list is exactly what the operator's Dhan Index-tab screenshots show (30) + SENSEX; if Dhan's tab carries more below the fold, it is a one-line edit to the allow-list. The brute-force ENGINE still sweeps only NIFTY/BANKNIFTY/SENSEX (CLAUDE.md §1/§3); the extra headline indices are REFERENCE in the lake. **Builds on:** DEC-DHAN-UNIVERSE-TICKVAULT-PARITY-001 (#366) · DEC-DHAN-VENDOR-SYMBOL-UNIQUE-001 (#364) · CLAUDE.md §1 (universe) / §3 (scope) / §11 COMMON.

368. **DEC-DHAN-COVERAGE-RESCOPE-001 — coverage ledger floor 2021 + in-scope underlyings only (drops the stale BSE-2020 grid)** (operator 2026-06-01 `make run`: "coverage also seems old" — the `/coverage` page showed a 4452-cell grid of **BSE single-stock underlyings from 2020**, status "missing"). A 3-agent audit (this session) root-caused it: (a) `_coverage_years()` (`app.py`) hardcoded `range(2020, …)` with a stale "Groww serves F&O from 2020" docstring — the locked floor is **2021-01-01** (DEC-DHAN-PULL-SCOPE-001 #348); (b) the `/api/v1/coverage` route derived its underlyings from `store.list_index_equity_instruments()` with **no scope filter**, so a **pre-#366 lake** still carrying BSE-equity rows surfaced them (the live selector is already NSE-only-for-stocks since #366, so it can't re-introduce them). **Fix:** floor → `range(2021, …)`; the coverage route now keeps every index (incl. BSE SENSEX) but only **NSE** equities (`inst.instrument_type is IDX or inst.exchange is NSE`) so the grid matches the live universe even before a fresh re-load (a re-load also prunes the stale rows via the replace-on-write snapshot). `app.py` is coverage-omitted glue; the `_coverage_years` floor is covered by the renamed `test_coverage_years_starts_2021`. Also swept the stale "from 2020"/"Groww" copy in `coverage.html`, `coverage_ledger.py`, `universe_phases.py` docstrings. **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** the route filter drops out-of-scope BSE *equities*; if a pre-#366 lake also carries non-allow-listed indices they persist in coverage until the next instrument re-load (which prunes them) — the durable fix is the re-load, the filter is the belt-and-braces. **Builds on:** DEC-DHAN-UNIVERSE-TICKVAULT-PARITY-001 (#366) · DEC-DHAN-INDEX-ALLOWLIST-001 (#367) · DEC-DHAN-PULL-SCOPE-001 (#348) · DEC-COVERAGE-LEDGER-001 · CLAUDE.md §3 (scope) / §11 COMMON.

369. **DEC-DB-VIEWER-NOSTORE-001 — `/db` browse fixed: serve the template fresh + `Cache-Control: no-store` (the "database not viewable" bug)** (operator 2026-06-01 `make run`: "database changes also not viewable not applicable"). A 3-agent audit (TestClient + headless-Chromium runtime check) proved the backend (`db_view.py`), the `/api/v1/db/*` routes, and the current `db.html` click→`/columns`→`/query`→render chain are **all correct in source** — clicking a table works end-to-end. The defect was a **stale served template**: `_load_db_template()` used a load-once module cache (`_DB_TEMPLATE_CACHE`), so a dashboard process started before the current `db.html` served the OLD page (whose click handler hit the removed `/api/v1/db/table/{name}`) for its whole lifetime, and the `/db` `HTMLResponse` carried no cache header so browsers could also serve a cached old page. **Fix:** `_load_db_template()` now reads `db.html` **fresh** on each request (no module cache) so a redeploy takes effect without a restart, and the `/db` route returns `HTMLResponse(..., headers={"Cache-Control": "no-store"})` so the browser never shadows it; the stale loader docstring (pre-#207 `/table/{name}` wording) was corrected. `app.py` is coverage-omitted glue; no logic/route-contract change. **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** only the `/db` template is de-cached + no-store here; the other 5 dashboard templates share the same load-once pattern and are a tracked follow-up (not user-flagged). **Builds on:** DEC-DB-VIEWER-001 · CLAUDE.md §11 COMMON.

370. **DEC-DHAN-INDEX-ALLOWLIST-002 — add Nifty Midcap 100 (`NIFTY MID100 FREE`) → 32 indices (tickvault parity)** (operator 2026-06-01, after reviewing the parallel tickvault index plan: tickvault locks 32 = 31 NSE + BSE SENSEX, including Nifty Midcap 100, which BruteX's #367 allow-list (30 NSE) omitted; operator chose "Add Nifty Midcap 100, keep BSE=SENSEX"). Adds the single token **`NIFTY MID100 FREE`** (Nifty Midcap 100, SECURITY_ID 37 — verified present in the real master) to `_ADMITTED_NSE_INDEX_SYMBOLS`, taking it to **31 NSE + BSE SENSEX = 32**. BSE stays **SENSEX-only** (NOT BANKEX/SENSEX50 — operator's standing rule; that is where BruteX deliberately diverges from tickvault's BSE set). Verified end-to-end against the real 220,287-row master: **32 indices + 211 stocks = 243**, zero `vendor_symbol` collisions. `instruments.py` stays **100% line+branch**; `test_nifty_midcap100_admitted_31_nse` asserts the symbol admits + the 31/32 counts. **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** the brute-force ENGINE still sweeps only NIFTY/BANKNIFTY/SENSEX (CLAUDE.md §1/§3); the 32 indices are the REFERENCE lake set. **Builds on:** DEC-DHAN-INDEX-ALLOWLIST-001 (#367) · DEC-DHAN-UNIVERSE-TICKVAULT-PARITY-001 (#366) · CLAUDE.md §1 (universe) / §3 (scope) / §11 COMMON.

371. **DEC-DHAN-GIFTNIFTY-SESSION-001 — GIFT Nifty is exempt from the 09:15–15:30 IST session filter** (operator 2026-06-01: "only for giftnifty alone we cant have the 9.15 am between 3.30 pm timestamp restriction"). GIFT Nifty trades on **NSE IX (GIFT City) for ~21 hours**, far outside the NSE regular session, so the `filter_intraday_session` window (DEC-SESSION-FILTER-001) would drop almost all of its 1-minute bars. **Fix (COMMON, one place):** `brutex/data/session.py` gains `SESSION_EXEMPT_SYMBOLS = {"GIFTNIFTY"}` + `is_session_exempt(trading_symbol)` (case-insensitive, whitespace-tolerant), and the spot ingest boundary (`adapters/dhan/ingest.py`) now skips the session filter for an exempt instrument: `if interval != _DAILY and not is_session_exempt(instrument.trading_symbol)`. Every other symbol (NIFTY/BANKNIFTY/SENSEX/stocks) keeps the strict `[09:15, 15:30)` filter unchanged. `session.py` + `ingest.py` stay **100% line+branch**; new `TestSessionExempt` (8 cases incl. case/whitespace/empty/None) + ingest tests proving GIFTNIFTY keeps an out-of-session 17:30-IST bar while NIFTY drops it. **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** the exemption is applied on the **spot** ingest path (the index candle feed the operator flagged); the rolling-option ingest still applies `in_session` per bar (GIFT Nifty options are not in the locked ATM±10 index-options fan-out, so no exemption is needed there yet — tracked if scope changes). The exempt set is one editable frozenset. **Builds on:** DEC-SESSION-FILTER-001 (#347) · DEC-DHAN-INDEX-ALLOWLIST-002 (#370) · DEC-DHAN-PULL-SCOPE-001 (#348) · CLAUDE.md §7 (market microstructure) / §11 COMMON.

372. **DEC-DHAN-OPEN-PCT-COLUMN-001 — `open_pct`: the per-candle 09:15-open % column** (operator 2026-06-01: "every candle gets one extra column = 9:15 AM open % change … the pre-market finalised price IS the 09:15 open price"). Adds `open_pct_series(bars)` to `brutex/data/spot_features.py` — the **intraday twin** of the existing `open_gap_pct` (DEC-SPOT-PCT-CHANGE-001 #346): where the open-gap uses the *previous* day's close as the base, this uses the **same** day's 09:15:00 IST session open. For each candle it returns `open_pct = (bar.close − today's 09:15 open) / 09:15 open × 100`, full precision (a statistical metric — **never money-rounded**, CLAUDE.md §4), as a list aligned 1:1 with the input bars. Reuses the COMMON `session_open_bar` + `pct_change` primitives (CLAUDE.md §11): when the day's 09:15 bar is absent there is no anchor → every entry is `None`; a `0` open → `None` for that bar (no divide-by-zero). `bars` is a single trading day's series (the caller groups by day). `spot_features.py` stays **100% line+branch**; **all testing types** added — unit (`TestOpenPctSeries`: anchor / 09:15-bar-self / no-anchor / zero-open / empty / full-precision) **+ property** (`tests/property/test_spot_features_properties.py`: 1:1 alignment, exact `pct_change` twin for any nonzero anchor, all-`None` without a 09:15 bar). **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** this lands the COMMON **computation** primitive (the same staging as `open_gap_pct`, which is also a pure feature awaiting its lake-write consumer); wiring `open_pct` as a persisted Parquet column + back-filling existing candles is the tracked follow-up. Applies to spot candles (the 3 brute-force indexes + INDIA VIX + stock spot), never options. **Builds on:** DEC-SPOT-PCT-CHANGE-001 (#346) · DEC-DHAN-GIFTNIFTY-SESSION-001 (#371) · CLAUDE.md §4 (no rounding stats) / §7 (09:15 session open) / §11 COMMON.

373. **DEC-FNO-UNIVERSE-BUILDER-001 — verified, timestamped F&O underlying-universe builder (NSE API ↔ `fo_mktlots` ↔ Dhan master)** (operator 2026-06-01: "Build a verified, timestamped F&O underlying universe … fetch live, never hardcode … fail loudly … no partial writes"). Self-contained (DEC-P0-026 — tickvault untouched). **Two pieces, COMMON-split:** **(1) `brutex/data/fno_universe.py`** — the pure, deterministic, **100%-tested** transforms: `parse_fo_mktlots` (the space-padded NSE lot-size CSV → near-month lot per F&O underlying, split at the `Derivatives on Individual Securities` marker into the 5 index underlyings vs the single-stock underlyings), `cross_check` / `common_symbols` (reconcile the three sources' symbol sets — every symbol present in one but missing from the others is surfaced, never silent), and `FnoUnderlying` + `to_csv` (the timestamped output rows: `symbol, underlying_security_id, instrument_type, exchange, lot_size, source, fetched_at_utc`). **(2) `scripts/build_fno_universe.py`** — the network glue (coverage-excluded; `source = ["brutex"]`): cookie-primes NSE (`GET /` → jar → `api/underlying-information` + `fo_mktlots.csv` with browser UA + Referer; archives fallback host), reuses BruteX's `INSTRUMENT_MASTER_DETAILED_URL` + `select_universe_report` to resolve the Dhan universe (stock→spot by integer `UNDERLYING_SECURITY_ID`, `*NSETEST` excluded, NSE-only stocks, indices = allow-list + SENSEX), cross-checks, then atomically writes `data/fno_underlyings_<YYYYMMDD>.csv` (tempfile + `replace` — no partial file) and **fails loudly** (non-zero exit) on any auth/short-response (`< 150` underlyings floor). **Verified offline:** `parse_fo_mktlots` on the operator's REAL `fo_mktlots.csv` yields exactly **5 index underlyings + 211 single-stock underlyings** (RELIANCE lot 500, NIFTY lot 65) — locked by a golden fixture test (`tests/fixtures/fo_mktlots_real.csv`), matching BruteX's 211-stock universe. `fno_universe.py` is **100% line+branch** (unit branch-coverage of every parse/skip path + cross-check + serialise). **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** the sandbox network policy **blocks `nseindia.com`** ("Host not in allowlist"), so the LIVE fetch + the actual `data/fno_underlyings_*.csv` must be generated by running the script on a machine with NSE access (the operator's Mac); the deterministic transforms + the real-fixture cross-check are proven here. **Builds on:** DEC-DHAN-UNIVERSE-DEDUP-001 (#365) · DEC-DHAN-INDEX-ALLOWLIST-002 (#370) · DEC-DHAN-PULL-SCOPE-001 (#348) · CLAUDE.md §1 / §6 (vendor facts) / §11 COMMON.

374. **DEC-DASHBOARD-INDEX-TESTS-ALIGN-001 — align 2 stale dashboard index-page assertions to locked design DEC-DASHBOARD-LIVE-FINALE (#234)** (2026-06-01, surfaced by the operator-requested deep workspace verification: "confirming the entire workspace works as expected"). The full test suite was re-run under a faithful **Python 3.12** environment (matching CI; `requires-python >=3.12`) with the Rust `fno_math` kernel + `[data,ui]` extras installed. Result: **3042 passed; package line+branch coverage 99.07%** (the actual CI gate is `--cov-fail-under=90`, which passes, plus the per-module strict-100% gates which pass; the ~0.93% gap is pre-existing legacy-orchestrator lines on the converge-to-100 path, NOT touched by this test-only change — the earlier "100%" wording here was an honest-envelope overclaim, corrected per Golden Rule #1/#10 in entry #376); **mypy --strict + pyright(0 errors) + interrogate(100%) clean** — with exactly **2 pre-existing stale dashboard assertions** failing. Root cause (verified pre-existing — they reproduce identically on the pre-#272 base and were never touched by PRs #272–#277): `brutex/dashboard/app.py:_render_index_html` documents the locked design **DEC-DASHBOARD-LIVE-FINALE (#234)** — the index page drives live updates via a **`/api/v1/state` poller (every 2s), not an inline `/ws` socket**, and the **Instruments Browser was moved out of the index into the `/api/v1/instruments` REST endpoint** (no longer an injected `instruments-card`). Two tests still asserted the superseded behaviour (`assert "/ws" in body`; `assert "instruments-card" in r.text`). These are **masked in CI** because the dashboard HTTP suite is guarded by `pytest.importorskip("httpx")` and `httpx` is in no CI extra — so the suite skips entirely on CI. **Fix:** `tests/integration/test_dashboard_e2e.py` now asserts the poller contract (`/api/v1/state` in the index body; the `/ws` *route* is still exercised by the websocket tests), and `tests/integration/test_dashboard_instruments.py::test_index_decouples_instruments_to_rest` now asserts the decoupling contract (the index never embeds `instruments-card`, even with a store wired, and `/api/v1/instruments` serves the rows). Test-only change — no `brutex/` source touched, so coverage/docstring gates are unaffected. **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** this aligns the tests with the locked design; it does **not** un-mask the dashboard suite in CI — doing so needs `httpx2` + a starlette pin (the installed starlette requires `httpx2`, not `httpx`) and is a separate dependency decision (tracked follow-up, deserves its own hostile review per §9). **Builds on:** DEC-DASHBOARD-LIVE-FINALE (#234) · DEC-FNO-UNIVERSE-BUILDER-001 (#373) · CLAUDE.md §9 / §11 COMMON.

375. **DEC-BENCH-RATE-LIMITER-FLAKE-001 — de-flake the `AdaptiveTokenBucket` O(1) acquire benchmark (finite-burst depletion under pytest-benchmark)** (2026-06-01, root-caused while monitoring PR #278's CI: the `Benchmarks (O(1) NFR gates)` job failed on `test_acquire_with_primed_window` with `assert 9.999999995125109e-05 == 0.0` — yet the *same* head SHA passed the aggregate on a parallel run, proving flake, not regression). Root cause: `tests/bench/test_rate_limiter_o1.py::_fresh_bucket` used `burst=1_000_000` with a frozen `FakeClock`; `pytest-benchmark` auto-calibrates its iteration count to the runner and, for a sub-microsecond `acquire`, can invoke `_call` **>1e6 times** within its time budget. With no clock advance there is no token refill, so a 1e6 burst can **drain on a fast runner** — at which point `acquire` *correctly* returns a tiny (~1e-4) throttle wait, flaking the exact `== 0.0` assertion. `AdaptiveTokenBucket._tokens` is a plain `float` counter (`rate_limiter.py:225` — no allocation proportional to burst), so the **fix** raises the bench burst to `1_000_000_000` (~1000× above any feasible benchmark iteration count) — the fast path now stays deterministically at zero wait, preserving the exact `== 0.0` contract (no assertion weakened). The genuine O(1) property is still independently enforced by `test_acquire_ratio_constant` (ratio < 5.0) and `test_aged_out_entries_do_not_slow_acquire` (< 100µs). Test-only change; verified deterministic across repeated local runs. **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** this hardens one flaky bench assertion; a broader sweep for other wall-clock-calibrated bench fragilities is a tracked follow-up. **Builds on:** DEC-DASHBOARD-INDEX-TESTS-ALIGN-001 (#374) · DEC-P0-020 (bench gates) · CLAUDE.md §2 Rule 5 (O(1) discipline) / §11 COMMON.

376. **DEC-DASHBOARD-CI-UNMASK-001 — un-mask the dashboard HTTP test suite on CI (install `[ui]` + httpx/httpx2) + honest-envelope coverage correction** (2026-06-01, operator-approved follow-up to the deep-verification finding: "Yes, un-mask it"). A 4-agent verification fan-out found that **4 dashboard test files** (`tests/integration/test_dashboard_e2e.py`, `test_dashboard_coverage.py`, `test_dashboard_instruments.py`, `tests/unit/test_dashboard_universe_routes.py`) were **silently skipped on CI**: the `test` job installed only `[dev,data]`, so every `pytest.importorskip("fastapi")` guard skipped, and `httpx` (needed by the `importorskip("httpx")` guard) plus `httpx2` (the backend Starlette's `TestClient` imports) were in no extra at all. Net hole: a PR that broke the dashboard HTTP layer could **auto-merge green** (this is exactly how the #374 stale assertions hid for so long). **Fix:** (1) `pyproject.toml` `[dev]` extra gains `httpx>=0.27,<1.0` + `httpx2>=2.0,<3.0`; (2) the CI `test` job install becomes `pip install -e ".[dev,data,ui]"`. With FastAPI + uvicorn + httpx + httpx2 present, all **36 dashboard tests now RUN and gate** (verified green locally under the faithful Python 3.12 env). `brutex/dashboard/app.py` remains coverage-omitted, so the package coverage gate is unaffected (still 99.07% ≥ the `--cov-fail-under=90` floor — the dashboard tests exercise routing without changing the gated denominator). **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #1/#10):** this entry also **corrects entry #374's "100% line+branch on `brutex/`" wording** to the true **99.07% package coverage** (CI gate `--cov-fail-under=90` ✅ + per-module strict-100 ✅; the ~0.93% gap is pre-existing legacy-orchestrator lines). Agent 4 also flagged a stale `[tool.coverage.run].omit` list (data/feed/strategy/execution runtime modules now installable in CI) — re-auditing that omit list to raise the real gated surface is a separate tracked follow-up, NOT done here. **Builds on:** DEC-DASHBOARD-INDEX-TESTS-ALIGN-001 (#374) · DEC-BENCH-RATE-LIMITER-FLAKE-001 (#375) · DEC-P0-001 (`[data]` in CI) · DEC-CLONE-RUN-001 (`[ui]` extra) · CLAUDE.md §9 / §11 COMMON.

377. **DEC-DASHBOARD-OMIT-AUDIT-001 — close the 4 remaining CI masking/omit holes + reconcile CLAUDE.md vendor docs Groww → Dhan** (2026-06-01, operator-approved follow-up to the 5-agent deep workspace verification: "make it ready for merge … and then go ahead with the plan"; operator selected all 4 CI fixes + "Fix CLAUDE.md to Dhan now"). The fan-out (5 parallel agents + sub-agents, run against merged main 23e7c10) returned: test-suite PASS (3042 passed / 0 failed), static/types/security PASS (ruff, mypy --strict, pyright 0 errors, bandit, vulture, interrogate 100%, jscpd), domain-correctness PASS (all 8 locked rules confirmed in code+tests), reproducibility 6/7 (look-ahead enforced via ts-cursor; named P-06 property still a Phase-2 stub), and a CI/merge-readiness hunt that surfaced 4 actionable holes beyond #279. **Fixes (this PR):** (1) **Coverage `omit` list un-audited (was stale):** the "needs [data]/[ui] not installed in CI" rationale is false post-DEC-P0-001 + #279 — both extras install in the CI `test` job. UN-OMITTED `feed/replay` (100%), `strategies/sweep` (99%), `strategies/runner` (98%), `data/sink` (96%), `data/parquet_writer` (93%), and `dashboard/app.py` (~75%, exercised by the now-un-skipped suite); their coverage now counts toward the 90% floor (**verified aggregate dropped 99.07% → 97.70%, still ≥ 90%**). `cli.py`, `__main__.py`, `data/catalog` (~86%), `data/instrument_store` (~87%), `execution/driver` (~83%) stay omitted (still below floor — dedicated-test follow-up). (2) **`tests/smoke` (12 tests) was wired into NO workflow + `tests/redteam` (32 gate-integrity meta-tests) was nightly-only:** both now run as a step in the per-PR `test` job (`pytest -q tests/smoke tests/redteam`, <5s combined, 44 passed), so a smoke break or a gate-detection regression fails the PR at merge time, not just nightly. (3) **`importorskip("httpx")` → `("httpx2")`** in `test_dashboard_e2e.py` — Starlette's TestClient backend is `httpx2`; the old guard could let the module run without httpx2 and raise RuntimeError instead of skipping. (4) **CLAUDE.md §1/§6 vendor reconciliation:** §6 still documented Groww, but the live vendor is **Dhan** per DEC-GROWW-DHAN-REVERT-001 (ADR-0002, 2026-05-31). Rewrote §1 data-source + §6 facts table from repo-sourced facts only (`brutex/adapters/dhan/constants.py` + `docs/dhan-ref/`, every row file:line-cited, no hallucination), fixed the stale active-branch + `feat(adapter/groww)` + directory references, and updated §2 Rule 1/3/6 to Dhan. **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** this does NOT lift the global floor to 100% (it stays 90%; aggregate is 97.70%), does NOT un-omit the 3 below-floor modules, does NOT implement the Phase-2 P-06 instrumented-accessor property, and does NOT change the O(1) bench tolerance (~5×–10×, not the decreed 1.1× — pre-existing §12 gap). Those remain tracked follow-ups. **Builds on:** DEC-DASHBOARD-CI-UNMASK-001 (#376) · DEC-GROWW-DHAN-REVERT-001 (ADR-0002) · DEC-P0-001 · CLAUDE.md §9 / §11 COMMON.

379. **DEC-PYRIGHT-UNMASK-001 — un-mask the pyright type gate: the CI `type` job now installs `[dev,data,ui]` (faithful type-checking of `brutex/dashboard/` + the data paths)** (2026-06-02, operator-chosen plan "harden the guarantees first"; closes the `[ui]`-masked-pyright follow-up flagged in #378's honest envelope + gap #3 of the 2026-06-02 verification report). **Root cause:** the CI `type` job installed `pip install -e ".[dev]"` only (`ci.yml`), so fastapi/pydantic/polars/duckdb resolved to Unknown under `reportMissingImports="none"` — pyright never actually type-checked the dashboard or data paths. With the runtime extras installed (the faithful env, matching the `test` job + local), `pyright brutex tests` surfaces **10 real errors that CI was masking**: 1 × `reportUntypedBaseClass` at `brutex/dashboard/app.py:80` (the `CredentialsIn(BaseModel)` pydantic-v2 model — a known pyright-strict false positive on pydantic's typed base) + 9 × `reportCallIssue` "Argument missing for parameter `draw`" in `tests/property/test_combo_hash_uniqueness.py` (the Hypothesis `@st.composite` strategies are called without `draw`, which Hypothesis injects — pyright does not model the `@composite` signature transform). **Fix:** (1) `ci.yml` type job → `pip install -e ".[dev,data,ui]"` (it already builds `fno_math`, DEC-P0-050), so pyright sees REAL types; (2) per-site `# pyright: ignore[reportUntypedBaseClass]` on `app.py:80` with rationale (auditable, blast-radius = that one pydantic model; the protected real-type-safety codes `reportCallIssue`/`reportArgumentType`/`reportAttributeAccessIssue`/`reportGeneralTypeIssues` stay hard errors per the DEC-P0-002 7-agent review); (3) file-scoped `# pyright: reportCallIssue=false` in `test_combo_hash_uniqueness.py` with a rationale (the `@composite` false-positive; scoped to that one file only); (4) **NEW regression guard** `tests/unit/test_ci_type_gate_faithful.py` — fails if the type job reverts to a `[dev]`-only install (prevents silent re-masking). **Verified locally (faithful env):** `pyright brutex tests` = **0 errors, 52 warnings** (the warnings are pre-existing non-blocking `reportPrivateUsage`; pyright exits 0); `mypy --strict brutex` clean (150 files); ruff + ruff-format clean; `test_combo_hash_uniqueness` 23 pass; new guard passes. **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #10):** this closes the type-gate masking hole; the 52 `reportPrivateUsage` warnings (whitebox tests touching private helpers) remain as warnings by design (`reportPrivateUsage="warning"`, DEC-P0-017), and the OTHER hardening items (O(1) 1.1× bench, omitted-module coverage, server-side branch protection, the int64-paisa §6 reconciliation) remain tracked. **Builds on:** DEC-DHAN-DOC-RECONCILE-001 (#378) · DEC-DASHBOARD-CI-UNMASK-001 (#376) · DEC-P0-002 / DEC-P0-014 (pyright strict policy) · CLAUDE.md §4 / §12 / §11 COMMON.

380. **DEC-PAISA-DOC-RECONCILE-001 — reconcile the "int64 paisa storage" docs to the verified-safe code (prices are exact `Decimal` rupees, not paisa)** (2026-06-02, operator-chosen plan "harden the guarantees first" → step 2; operator selected "Fix the docs to match the code"). **Verified ground truth:** OHLC / spot / option **prices** are parsed to exact `Decimal` rupees via `brutex/data/candle_fields.py::to_price` (`Decimal(str(raw))` — no float drift, non-finite rejected), stored as `Decimal` on `CandleBar`/`OptionBar` and as Parquet `Float64` (`brutex/data/parquet_writer.py`), and `brutex/data/models.py:142-144` explicitly documents *"prices are exact Decimal rupees … the paisa ×100 happens at the lake-write boundary, never here"*. **int64 paisa is real only at the cost + display boundary** (`brutex/costs/` + `brutex/money.py`), NOT for price storage. But three docs overstated it: CLAUDE.md §4 ("Internal storage stays int64 paisa"), CLAUDE.md §6 ("Price unit … parser MUST × 100 at parse boundary to preserve int64 paisa", with a **wrong citation** — `dhan/parser.py:61-64` is the array-extraction helper `_as_array`, not a ×100), and `brutex/money.py:3` ("Track 1 stores every price as int64 paisa"). **Fix (docs-to-code, per the operator choice — NOT a code refactor, since `Decimal` is already exact and a paisa migration would churn reproducibility hashes for no precision gain):** corrected CLAUDE.md §4 + §6 (accurate text + real citations: `candle_fields.to_price`→`Decimal`, `models.py:142-144`) and the `money.py` module docstring (money/costs = int64 paisa; prices = Decimal rupees). **NEW property test** `tests/property/test_price_precision_invariant.py` (8 cases) — pins that `to_price` returns the exact rupee `Decimal`, never a ×100 paisa integer, and rejects non-finite; fails if a paisa-scaling regression enters the price path. **Verified locally:** the 8 property cases pass; `pyright` on the new test 0 errors (the faithful gate from #379); `mypy --strict brutex` clean; ruff + ruff-format clean; interrogate 100% (gated). **Honest envelope (Golden Rule #1/#10):** this corrects an inaccurate factual claim in the authority doc to match the code (the code was right + numerically safe all along); it does **not** change any runtime behavior or storage format, and does not touch the still-tracked hardening items (O(1) 1.1× bench, omitted-module coverage, server-side branch protection). If the operator later wants TRUE int64-paisa price storage, that is a separate, larger decision (needs a 4-agent hostile review per §9). **Builds on:** DEC-PYRIGHT-UNMASK-001 (#379) · DEC-MONEY-2DP-001 · CLAUDE.md §4 / §6 / §11 COMMON.
